Abstract: We develop an operator-algebraic framework for analyzing fault tolerance in quantum circuits and establish necessary and sufficient conditions for fault tolerance under any given noise model. For prescribed circuit families and noise models, we derive a semidefinite program (SDP) based test whose infeasibility certifies that there does not exist any recovery map restoring its intended operation. For non-adaptive stabilizer circuits, analyzing the stabilizer symmetries of its components with symplectic geometry, we obtain closed-form detection and logical-effect matrices that characterize circuit distance algebraically. Applied to a finite Hastings--Haah honeycomb Floquet-code circuit, these matrices certify the dynamical encoding of two logical qubits and distance four under Pauli noise including measurement errors. Using these matrices, we then derive MacWilliams identities for circuit weight enumerators and obtain linear-programming (LP) upper bounds on circuit distance. Output-code constraints give additional Singleton-like bounds. Under independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) depolarizing noise, the weight enumerators determine decoder failure probabilities and yield upper bounds on finite-circuit pseudothresholds. Finally, we derive LP distance bounds for families of flag syndrome-extraction circuits specified by their CNOT orderings, by incorporating the $t$-flag criterion as additional linear constraints to the distance-bounding LP. An analytical CSS Hamming-code benchmark yields a tight distance-three bound attained by the Chao--Reichardt one-flag construction.

</details>


### [82] [An exact semidefinite characterization of the stoquasticity cone of permutationally invariant Bell operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03438)
*Jan Li,Owidiusz Makuta,Evert van Nieuwenburg,Jordi Tura*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在热力学极限下，任意阶置换不变Bell算子的"stoquasticity cone"（随机性锥）具有精确的半定表征：沿每条非对角线，矩阵元是同一个单变量多项式的取值，故该条件等价于单个线性矩阵不等式（LMI），无需松弛层级，判定stoquasticity即一个半定可行性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原子系综中已用置换不变（PI）Bell算子探测到Bell关联，且迄今实现的所有算子，其最大违背态在适当基下可化为非负振幅形式，相应Bell算子为stoquastic。刻画这些算子的系数（即stoquasticity cone）此前仅对两体算子完成；对多体算子，不等式数量随系统规模n增长，超两体情形一直悬而未决。

Method: 关键观察：在每条非对角线上，PI Bell算子的矩阵元是同一个单变量多项式的取值。由于是单变量，该（非负）条件等价于一个线性矩阵不等式，不存在松弛层级。由此将stoquasticity判定转化为单个半定可行性问题，并在热力学极限下对任意算子阶数给出精确的半定刻画。

Result: 在热力学极限下，stoquasticity cone获得对任意算子阶数都成立的精确半定表征。作为最低阶实例，该描述恢复了两体算子已知的三超平面刻画；对三体算子，则给出弯曲（曲面型）边界，而非有限个超平面。

Conclusion: stoquasticity cone在多体、任意阶情形下可被单一LMI精确刻画，克服了不等式数目随n增长的困难，无需松弛层级；结果统一并推广了已有的两体刻画，并揭示三体算子起边界呈弯曲形态。

Abstract: Bell correlations have been detected in atomic ensembles ranging from hundreds to hundreds of thousands of particles, witnessed by the permutationally invariant (PI) Bell operators. For all the operators realized so far, the maximally violating state can be brought, in a suitable basis, into a nonnegative-amplitude form. In that basis, the Bell operator is stoquastic. The coefficients parametrising these operators that at the same time lead to stoquasticity form a convex cone, the so-called stoquasticity cone. Its characterization had remained open beyond two-body operators, as for many-body operators the number of inequalities grows with the system size $n$. In this work we show that, in the thermodynamic limit, the stoquasticity cone admits an exact semidefinite characterization, valid at any operator order. The key observation is that, along each off-diagonal, the matrix elements are the values of a single univariate polynomial. Being univariate, this condition is equivalent to a linear matrix inequality, with no relaxation hierarchy. Therefore, deciding stoquasticity becomes a single semidefinite feasibility problem. As its lowest-degree instances, this description recovers the known three-hyperplane characterization for two-body operators and yields, for three-body operators, a curved boundary.

</details>

*Lucio De Simone,Lorenzo Capra,Arthur Vesperini,Leonardo Rossi,Loris Di Cairano,Roberto Franzosi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文从Fubini–Study度量诱导的黎曼几何角度研究多量子比特纠缠，提出纠缠距离（ED）这一几何度量，并在两量子比特纯态下证明其等价于concurrence与纠缠熵的单调函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠是量子信息理论的核心资源，但多体系统中纠缠的一般刻画与量化仍具挑战性。作者希望借助投影希尔伯特空间上的几何结构，为纠缠提供统一的几何解释。

Method: 利用多量子比特态投影希尔伯特空间上Fubini–Study度量的局域酉不变性，将纠缠定义为局域操作生成的平方Fubini–Study距离之和无法被局域最小化的障碍，从而导出纠缠距离（ED）。分析纯多量子比特态ED的性质及其在局域操作与经典通信下的行为。

Result: ED在一定限制条件下可重现已有纠缠度量：对两量子比特纯态，ED分别等价于concurrence的精确单调函数以及纠缠熵的显式单调函数；同时指出此类对应关系在两量子比特之外存在局限。

Conclusion: ED为两体标准纠缠度量提供了清晰的几何解释，但该几何对应关系难以直接推广到更一般的多体情形。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement is a fundamental resource in quantum information theory, yet its general characterization and quantification remain challenging, especially in multipartite systems. In this work we investigate entanglement from a geometric perspective, focusing on the Riemannian structure induced by the Fubini--Study metric on the projective Hilbert space of multi-qubit quantum states. By exploiting the local-unitary invariance of this metric, we derive the entanglement distance (ED), a geometric measure that quantifies entanglement as an obstruction to locally minimizing the sum of squared Fubini--Study distances generated by local operations.
  We analyze the properties of ED for pure multi-qubit states and discuss its behavior under local operations and classical communication. In particular, we show that ED reproduces established entanglement measures in well-defined and restricted settings. For pure states of two qubits, ED reduces to an exact monotone function of the concurrence and, independently, to an explicit monotone function of the entropy of entanglement. These results provide a clear geometric interpretation of standard bipartite entanglement measures within the present framework, while highlighting the limitations of such correspondences beyond the two-qubit case.

</details>

*Zhen Qin,Qing Qu,Alfred O. Hero*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 在上下文学习框架下，为基于Transformer的神经量子态建立理论分析框架，推导均方误差（MSE）意义下的推理时泛化误差界；并进一步扩展至以秩一密度算子表示的完整量子态，在连续与离散域、物理约束下给出基于MSE的泛化界。

Result: 建立了严格的推理时MSE泛化误差界，表明逐点预测误差与上下文样本数及Transformer深度均呈反比下降；达到该保证所需的Transformer深度仅随系统规模（连续系统的粒子数或离散系统的量子比特数）线性增长；数值模拟结果与理论分析相符。

Conclusion: 该工作首次从理论上刻画了Transformer型神经量子态的泛化行为，揭示了深度与系统规模的线性scaling关系，并推广至连续与离散域下的完整量子态，为相关模型的表达能力与可扩展性提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Neural quantum states based on modern deep learning architectures have emerged as powerful representations for quantum many-body systems. In particular, Transformer-based neural quantum states provide expressive models capable of capturing long-range correlations, and their empirical generalization performance has recently been demonstrated. However, a theoretical understanding of their generalization behavior remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we develop a theoretical framework to analyze the generalization properties of Transformer-based neural quantum states under in-context learning. We establish a rigorous inference-time generalization error bound in terms of mean squared error (MSE), showing that the pointwise prediction error decreases inversely with both the number of in-context examples and the depth of the Transformer. We further show that the Transformer depth required to achieve this guarantee scales only linearly with the system size--namely, the number of particles in continuous systems or the number of qudits in discrete systems. Building on this result, we extend our analysis to full quantum states formulated as rank-one density operators, and derive MSE-based generalization bounds over both continuous and discrete domains under physical constraints. Finally, numerical simulations corroborate our theoretical analysis.

</details>

*Ryota Matsuda,Masahiro Hoshino,Yuto Ashida*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于矩阵乘积态（MPS）的完美采样方法，用于高效计算二阶magic Rényi熵（MRE），从而定量评估多体费米子非高斯性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费米子非高斯性是通用量子计算的关键资源，但现有的基于卷积的MRE度量因计算成本随系统尺寸指数增长而仅适用于小系统，无法评估大尺度多体系统中的非高斯性。

Method: 开发完美采样方法从MPS直接计算二阶MRE：采用有界估计量控制采样波动，并通过递归扫描直接从输入MPS中抽取样本，每个样本生成时间为O(nD³)，其中n为模式数，D为MPS键维数。

Conclusion: 该方法为定量评估多体费米子非高斯性提供了计算工具，能够计入高阶关联，突破了以往方法在系统规模上的限制。

Abstract: Fermionic non-Gaussianity is a key resource for universal quantum computation, and its behavior in quantum many-body systems has attracted growing interest. Recently, a convolution-based measure, which we call the magic Rényi entropy (MRE), has been proposed as a resource measure of non-Gaussianity, but its evaluation is limited to small systems due to the computational cost growing exponentially with system size. In this work, we develop a perfect-sampling method to calculate the second-order MRE from matrix product states (MPSs). Our method uses a bounded estimator to control sampling fluctuations and a recursive sweep to draw the samples directly from the input MPS, which allows us to generate each sample with $\mathcal O(nD^3)$ time, where $n$ and $D$ denote the number of modes and MPS bond dimension, respectively. We benchmark our method on the XXZ chain with up to $128$ sites, demonstrating that the MRE captures intricate higher-order correlations at large scales, which are inaccessible to covariance-based measures. These results provide a computational tool that quantitatively evaluates many-body fermionic non-Gaussianity by accounting for higher-order correlations.

</details>

Result: 单边强度图样显示出保留的模间相位，联合拟合给出像素不对称度0.4%对47%，与不相干模式混合不符；被动光纤的有效芯间耦合仅由发射决定，可在3.9%-13.1%间调谐；预告|2>态的每光子串扰与|1>相同，将Fock态依赖性上界约束到|dx/x|<6.1%（95%置信水平），|1>与|2>的成像模式分布一致度优于1%；并展示若未扣除偶然符合，基于符合的事件选择会产生虚假的光子数依赖串扰。

Conclusion: 该工作在电信多芯光纤中首次启用纤芯内空间模式作为量子通道，证明了模间相干性保持、发射可调耦合以及传播的线性光学无光子数依赖性，为光纤量子行走中真正的多光子干涉认证提供了基线，并提醒符合计数事件选择需正确扣除偶然符合。

Abstract: Multicore optical fibers (MCFs), developed to scale classical telecommunications, are gaining traction as a quantum platform for high-dimensional key distribution, entanglement, and photonic quantum walks. So far, quantum light has occupied these fibers only as single-mode path channels; the spatial-mode structure within each core has remained untouched. Here we operate a telecom-grade 39-core fiber far from its design wavelength, where each core supports exactly two LP mode groups (LP01, LP11), and measure heralded photon-number states |1> and |2>, resolving the photon number simultaneously with the intra-core spatial mode or with the core index. Displacing the launch prepares a coherent LP01-LP11 superposition whose single-sided intensity pattern shows that the intermodal phase is preserved over the fiber: a drift-resolved joint fit gives a pixel asymmetry (0.4% vs 47%) incompatible with an incoherent mode mixture. Because the two modes couple to neighboring cores at different rates (kappa_LP11/kappa_LP01 = 2.4), the launched mode content acts as a state-selectable coupling knob: the effective inter-core coupling of the passive fiber is set by the launch alone (3.9-13.1%), a tunability that otherwise requires electro-optic, thermo-optic, or mechanical reconfiguration. The propagation is photon-number independent: the per-photon crosstalk of heralded |2> states equals that of |1>, bounding any Fock-state dependence to |dx/x| < 6.1% (95% CL), and the imaged mode distributions of |1> and |2> agree to better than 1%. This measured linear-optics null, validated against exact Fock-space simulation, is the baseline against which genuine multiphoton interference in fiber quantum walks must be certified; we also show how coincidence-based event selection produces spurious photon-number-dependent crosstalk if accidentals are not subtracted.

</details>

Method: 采用阈值量子态层析（threshold quantum state tomography），借助利用高度纠缠双光子频率梳稀疏性的自适应测量策略，并结合贝叶斯推断完成全态估计。

Abstract: Frequency-bin encoding provides a scalable platform for photonic quantum information processing by enabling high-dimensional qudit states compatible with the telecommunications infrastructure. However, characterizing these systems remains challenging, as conventional quantum state tomography requires measurement resources that increase rapidly with dimensionality, compounded by the difficulty of implementing controllable measurements across many frequency modes at once. Here, we address these challenges and demonstrate threshold quantum state tomography of entangled frequency-bin qudits using adaptive measurements that exploit the sparsity of highly entangled biphoton frequency combs. Combined with Bayesian inference, we experimentally demonstrate full state estimation of biphoton frequency combs with Hilbert space dimensions up to $d^2=100$---a 56% increase over the previous record---all while performing a mere 1.9% of the measurements required by conventional tomographically complete approaches. This work confirms the promise of measurement-aware approaches for characterizing high-dimensional photonic quantum systems, unlocking new tools for full tomography that are hardware-efficient, state-aware, and free from a priori assumptions about state structure.

### [88] [Breaking the chain: geometry-native state preparation with ASPIRE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03528)
*Fredrik Hasselgren,Matthew L. Sims-Goh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: ASPIRE 是一种基于纠缠几何的 MPS 近似态制备编译算法，通过迭代移除纠缠、选择可并行长程门，实现更高保真度与更浅电路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态加载是阻碍端到端量子优势的关键瓶颈，尤其对矩阵乘积态（MPS）等结构化状态。现有近似制备方法多依赖最近邻门的顺序阶梯结构，难以适配长程纠缠与处理器有限连通性。

Method: 提出 ASPIRE（Adaptive State Preparation by Iterative Removal of Entanglement）。该算法依据状态的纠缠几何与目标处理器的连通性选择门，对候选量子比特对按其纠缠可移除性进行评分，识别能移除最多纠缠的可并行长程门层，从而自适应地编译态制备电路。

Result: 在多种设置下实证验证，可成功制备哈密顿量基态与多元振幅编码函数，并考虑量子比特连通性、硬件噪声以及 NISQ 与早期容错场景。对于长程纠缠态，ASPIRE 通常比现有方法获得更高保真度和更浅电路，即使计入经典优化开销亦然。

Conclusion: ASPIRE 是一种有竞争力且资源节约的态制备协议，特别适合容忍不完美保真度的应用，如量子相位估计的引导态制备。

Abstract: Loading data into quantum computers is a key bottleneck that threatens end-to-end quantum advantage. Quantum-circuit compilers for structured states like matrix product states (MPS) are a critical enabler to manage this in quantum chemistry, finance, and many-body quantum simulation. Approximate preparation of MPS typically relies on layered sequential staircases of nearest-neighbour gates. We present Adaptive State Preparation by Iterative Removal of Entanglement (ASPIRE), an algorithm that approximately compiles state-preparation circuits by selecting gates based on the state's underlying entanglement geometry and the available connectivity of the target quantum processor. Our protocol scores candidate qubit pairs on the removability of their entanglement and identifies layers of parallelised long-ranged gates that remove the most entanglement. We demonstrate our protocol empirically across a range of settings, illustrating its success for preparing Hamiltonian ground states and multivariate amplitude-encoded functions, and accounting for qubit connectivity, hardware noise, and both NISQ and early fault-tolerant settings. Throughout, ASPIRE typically achieves higher fidelities and shallower circuits than existing methods for states with long-ranged entanglement, even when classical optimisation is accounted for. Our investigation positions ASPIRE as a competitive, resource-frugal state-preparation protocol, with particular promise for applications tolerant to imperfect fidelity such as preparation of guide states for quantum phase estimation.

</details>


### [89] [What Must a Quantum-Memory Decoder Know About Temporally Correlated Noise?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03545)
*Danesh Morales-Hashemi,Graeme Smith,Ningping Cao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究量子纠错解码器对时间关联噪声必须知道什么信息、以及学习这些信息需要多大代价，以已知系统-环境相互作用的固定稳定子存储实验为对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错解码通常依赖对噪声模型的了解，但噪声在时间上关联时，解码器究竟需要哪些信息、缺失信息的代价如何，尚不清楚，本文旨在量化这一"校准"问题。

Method: 将校准表述为选择泡利恢复所需的保真度差异，把缺失信息与保真度损失关联；主例采用一类CSS码（含Shor、Steane、奇距离旋转表面码）中的弱相干X旋转，对比运行内固定符号与每间隔独立重抽样两种模型，并区分是否保留随机泡利操作记录。

Result: 两模型具有相同的综合征统计与相同的时空泡利过程(SPP)，但在合适存储时间下需要不同的逻辑修正；弱噪声极限下，若模型已知几乎可完美纠正，而模型未知时即使使用完整综合征记录，最优最终恢复的最坏情况保真度损失仍趋近1/2。对固定码和固定低于1/2的目标损失，证明了匹配的校准界：完整QEC周期所需噪声间隔数按旋转角的倒数、以码的X距离为幂次缩放；允许提取前两个不间断间隔可将最优代价降至平方反比缩放。对每个间隔做物理泡利扭转会同时改变恢复性能与信息需求；丢弃随机泡利记录使SPP对最优恢复充分，保留该记录可提升可达保真度但可能需额外噪声信息。

Conclusion: 时间关联噪声下解码器的信息需求可被精确量化：模型对比可产生相同的可观测量却需要不同的逻辑修正，缺失信息导致高达1/2的保真度损失，并存在与码距离相关的校准成本标度律；泡利扭转与操作记录的取舍会改变最优恢复的信息充分性与可达性能。

Abstract: What must a quantum error-correction decoder know about temporally correlated noise, and what does it cost to learn? We study this question for fixed stabilizer memory experiments with known system-environment interactions. We formulate calibration in terms of the fidelity differences that select a Pauli recovery and relate missing information to the resulting fidelity loss. Our main example uses weak coherent $X$ rotations in a class of CSS codes including Shor, Steane, and odd-distance rotated surface codes. All physical qubits share a sign either fixed throughout a run or independently redrawn each interval. The two models have identical syndrome statistics and the same spatiotemporal Pauli process (SPP), obtained by Pauli-twirling every noise interval. Yet at suitable storage times, they require different logical corrections. In the weak-noise limit, each model is almost perfectly correctable when known. Without model information, even the best final recovery has a worst-case fidelity loss approaching $1/2$ relative to the noise-aware optimum, despite access to the full syndrome record. For this pair of models, at fixed code and fixed positive target loss below $1/2$, we prove matching calibration bounds. With logical preparation and final readout, complete QEC cycles require a number of noise intervals scaling as the inverse rotation angle raised to the code's $X$ distance. Allowing two uninterrupted intervals before one extraction reduces the optimal cost to inverse-square scaling. Physical Pauli twirling of every noise interval changes both recovery performance and its information requirements. Discarding the record of applied random Pauli operations makes the SPP sufficient for optimal recovery. Keeping this record can improve the attainable fidelity, but reaching that optimum may require additional noise information.

</details>


### [90] [A graph-theoretic analysis of non-generic free-fermion solvability by Krylov decompositions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03557)
*Jannis Ruh,Samuel J. Elman,Adrian Chapman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作通过"Krylov流"算子将Fendley的FFD链拓展为一类仅在精细调节耦合下可解的模型，并证明其与 frustration graph 的诱导路径结构及高斯变换的完全特殊正交代数相对应，从而把"路径支撑"确定为非泛型可解性的标志。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子自旋哈密顿量中的可解模型通常由其相互作用的结构特征（如反对易泡利项的 frustration graph）来识别，这些图刻画了可在任意或一般耦合值下映射到自由费米子的大类自旋模型。然而，还存在只在精细调节耦合时才具有自由费米子解的非泛型可解模型。本文旨在用图结构刻画这一类模型，从而系统性地发现精确可解的多体哈密顿量。

Method: 以Fendley的自由费米子伪装（FFD）链的任意实例为出发点，构造一组称为Krylov currents的算子。这些算子在有效费米子模式下严格为二次型，因此可将FFD链拓展为一个自由费米子可解哈密顿量的线性族。由于Krylov currents对FFD哈密顿量项是非线性的，所生成族中的元素属于非泛型可解模型，不满足已知的泛型可解性条件。作者进一步证明每个Krylov current都可以按frustration graph的诱导路径展开，并且这些算子生成高斯变换的完全特殊正交代数。

Result: 证明了任意Krylov current均可表示为frustration graph诱导路径的展开；这些Krylov currents生成高斯变换的完整特殊正交（special-orthogonal）代数；由此得到的哈密顿量线性族中的元素在耦合精细调节时才可解，且不满足已知的泛型可解条件。

Conclusion: 路径支撑（path support）可作为非泛型可解性的识别标志；Krylov currents提供了系统性寻找精确可解多体哈密顿量的途径。

Abstract: Exactly solvable models are often identified from structural features of their interactions. For quantum spin Hamiltonians, these are captured by the frustration graph of anticommuting Pauli terms. The frustration graph characterizes broad families of spin models that map to free fermions for arbitrary, or generic, values of their coupling strengths. Nevertheless, there exist non-generically solvable models that admit free-fermion solutions only at finely tuned couplings. In this work, we characterize a family of such models in terms of graph structures. Given an arbitrary instance of Fendley's free-fermions-in-disguise (FFD) chain, we construct a set of operators, called Krylov currents, that are exactly quadratic in its effective fermionic modes and thus extend it to a linear family of free-fermion solvable Hamiltonians. As the Krylov currents are nonlinear in the FFD Hamiltonian terms, the elements of this generated family are non-generically solvable, and they accordingly fail the known conditions for generic solvability. We prove that every Krylov current admits an expansion in terms of induced paths of the frustration graph and that they generate the full special-orthogonal algebra of Gaussian transformations. Our results identify path support as a signature of non-generic solvability, and they provide a systematic route to finding exactly solvable many-body Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [91] [Gibbs state preparation through quantum decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03566)
*Christophe Piveteau,Yihui Quek,M. Isabel Franco Garrido*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 QD-HDQI 算法，用量子解码器替代 HDQI 的经典相干解码器，利用不同错误的干涉来降低 Gibbs 态制备的可达温度阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs 态制备是量子计算与统计物理的核心任务。HDQI 将 Pauli 哈密顿量的 Gibbs 态制备归约为纠错码解码，但其相干经典解码器无法利用不同错误之间的干涉，导致可达逆温度受限（在可纠正错误比例上仅为线性），因此需要引入量子解码来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 以 HDQI 为出发点，用可处理错误间干涉的量子解码器替换其经典相干解码器，构造 QD-HDQI。对交换 Pauli 哈密顿量，将归约化为在 i.i.d. 纯态经典-量子信道上传输相应辛码；对均匀随机哈密顿量符号，算法可达最低温度由高效解码器的零误差阈值决定。研究两类量子解码器：基于无歧义态区分（USD）的解码器，以及带量子消息的信念传播（BPQM）。

Result: 对交换 Hamiltonian，归约高效；即使用经典解码器，可达逆温度也从可纠正错误比例的线性提升到平方根级别，量子解码器可容忍更强噪声而进一步改善。USD 解码器对任意二元线性码有效，但对其在交换 Hamiltonian 下被"去量子化"，给出温度阈值相匹配的经典拒绝采样器。对某些稀磁自旋玻璃，BPQM 的解码阈值超过经典解码的 Shannon 极限，达到框架内任何经典解码器无法触及的温度。

Conclusion: QD-HDQI 通过量子解码器显著扩展了 Gibbs 态制备的可达温度范围，表明量子解码在某些体系中具有超越经典解码 Shannon 极限的优势，同时也揭示了部分量子策略可被经典拒绝采样去量子化。

Abstract: We propose a Gibbs state preparation algorithm that exploits algebraic structure in the Hamiltonian. Our starting point is Hamiltonian Decoded Quantum Interferometry (HDQI), a recent algorithm that reduces Gibbs state preparation for Pauli Hamiltonians to decoding error-correcting codes. We introduce quantum decoding HDQI (QD-HDQI), replacing HDQI's coherent classical decoder by a quantum decoder that can exploit interference between different errors.
  For commuting Pauli Hamiltonians, our reduction is efficient and the associated quantum decoding problem corresponds to transmitting the associated symplectic code over i.i.d. pure-state classical-quantum channels. For uniformly random Hamiltonian signs, the vanishing-error threshold of an efficient decoder directly determines the lowest temperature that our algorithm can reach. Even using only classical decoders, this improves upon HDQI's achievable inverse temperature from linear to square-root in the correctable error fraction; quantum decoders can improve further by tolerating still stronger noise. A similar picture extends to the noncommuting regime previously handled by HDQI, where now the noise is correlated within small, independent blocks.
  We study two such quantum decoders in our QD-HDQI Gibbs sampling framework. The first, based on unambiguous state discrimination, works efficiently for arbitrary binary linear codes. However, we "dequantize" this strategy for commuting Hamiltonians, contributing a classical rejection sampler that matches its temperature threshold. The second is belief propagation with quantum messages (BPQM), a quantum generalization of classical belief propagation. We show that for certain dilute spin glasses, BPQM's decoding threshold exceeds the Shannon limit for classical decoding, reaching temperatures inaccessible to any classical decoder within our framework.

</details>


### [92] [Rapid mixing of Gibbs samplers via quantum Dobrushin--Shlosman conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03578)
*Cambyse Rouzé,Daniel Stilck França*

TL;DR: 该论文将经典Dobrushin–Shlosman块更新理论推广到非对易量子格点系统，提出"平滑热浴动力学"及其量子Dobrushin–Shlosman条件，证明有限程量子自旋链在任意固定有限温度下具对数迹范数混合，并给出高效制备Gibbs态的量子算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Dobrushin–Shlosman理论通过热浴块更新给出Gibbs采样器快速混合的尖锐温度阈值，且其混合保证常强于更严格的单点条件。然而该方法尚缺乏面向非对易量子格点系统的对应理论，难以刻画有限块量子动力学的快速混合与Gibbs态制备复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了量子版Dobrushin–Shlosman理论，其有限时间弛豫机制在经典热浴块更新中没有对应物，为量子Gibbs采样器的快速混合与高效制备提供了统一判据与复杂度保证。

Abstract: Classical Dobrushin--Shlosman theory uses heat-bath block updates to identify sharp temperature thresholds for establishing the rapid mixing of Gibbs samplers. In particular, these mixing guarantees often hold even when stricter single-site conditions fail. We extend this approach to noncommuting quantum lattice systems through a quantum Dobrushin--Shlosman condition for finite-block dynamics, which we call smoothed heat-bath dynamics. We establish this condition in two regimes. For finite-range quantum spin chains at every fixed finite temperature, we prove logarithmic trace-norm mixing in system size, with normalized bounds uniform in on-site fields and single exponential in inverse temperature. We also prove stability under small local perturbations of interacting, possibly noncommuting reference Hamiltonians on graphs of polynomial volume growth. For classical references with bounded local interactions, uniform strong spatial mixing implies our quantum condition. With bounded local terms and fixed physical, geometric and block parameters, the resulting samplers prepare Gibbs states to trace-norm error $\varepsilon$ using $N\operatorname{polylog}(N/\varepsilon)$ gates and classical operations.
  Our block updates combine a local Gibbs reset with quantum belief propagation to incorporate interactions across the block boundary, yielding exactly Gibbs-preserving, KMS-reversible channels. Two independently tunable parameters control contraction: an internal evolution time suppresses the influence of sites inside a block, while the block size allows interior contraction to dominate boundary influence. This finite-time relaxation has no counterpart in a classical heat-bath block update.

</details>

Abstract: For classical--quantum wiretap channels with a transitive covariant input action, both output ensembles admit a description as unitary group orbits of fixed seed states. We use this structure to construct finite-block preprocessing schemes whose private information is an entropy difference evaluated at two distributions related by group convolution. Applying the construction to product groups allows correlations across any fixed number of channel uses. We give a sufficient condition for the associated uniform-input bound to equal the single-use private information. Constant-composition channel codes and universal$_2$ hashing achieve the resulting rates, with trace-distance leakage controlled by sandwiched Rényi information. For uniform outer randomization, the representation structure reduces the comparison-state optimization to Eve's invariant states. For the hybrid binary channel considered by Tikku, Berta and Renes, numerical optimization at three and four uses gives rates above our numerical estimate of the optimized noisy-repetition benchmark wherever that estimate is positive on the tested grid. The improvement comes from noise distributions beyond the family of independent physical flips and a symmetric logical flip. We also prove nonadditivity at an explicit channel parameter using a two-use encoder, an analytic single-use converse, and certified entropy bounds. Finally, we relate the hybrid model to binary phase-shift keying with a fixed receiver measurement.

</details>


### [94] [Quantum algorithms for orthogonal polynomial transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03581)
*Anupam Prakash,Shree Hari Sureshbabu,Dylan Herman,Shouvanik Chakrabarti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一系列高效量子正交多项式变换（QOPT）算法，覆盖Askey方案中的离散与连续多项式族，时间复杂度达O(polylog(N,1/ε))或亚二次，并建立光学门编译与快速转发新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Hermite变换已有高效实现，但Askey方案中其他正交多项式族的量子变换（离散Charlier、Meixner、Krawtchouk，连续Laguerre、Jacobi、Hahn等）缺乏高效算法，限制了量子算法在涉及这些变换任务的加速能力。

Method: 利用Askey方案多项式与高斯量子光学门之间的关联，扩展Iyer等人的编译框架，为笛卡尔网格上的SU(2)与SU(1,1)光学门建立编译器；将连续Jacobi变换归约为离散Hahn变换，并给出SU(2)表示耦合（自旋(j1,j2)）Clebsch–Gordan变换的高效编译；针对任意阶ν>0的Laguerre变换，通过3项chirp分解快速转发径向振子，并在对数网格上设计量子Hankel变换算法。

Result: 离散Charlier、Meixner、Krawtchouk及连续整数阶Laguerre变换实现O(polylog(N,1/ε))时间；Hahn变换达到O(N·polylog((N+α+β+1)/ε))，相比朴素实现实现二次加速；并构建了适用于任意阶ν>0的Laguerre变换框架。

Conclusion: 通过统一光学门编译与快速转发技术，将高效量子正交多项式变换推广至Askey方案多数多项式族，实现了对数多项式或近线性时间复杂度，显著优于朴素方法。

Abstract: A quantum orthogonal polynomial transform (QOPT) is an algorithmic primitive that maps a superposition of standard basis states $\sum_k α_{k} \ket{k}$ coherently to a basis of normalized univariate polynomials orthogonal with respect to a probability measure $μ(x)$. We provide new discrete and continuous QOPTs for polynomial families in the Askey scheme extending the results for the quantum Hermite transform (Jain et al., STOC'26). Our efficient QOPT algorithms require time $O(\text{polylog}(N, 1/ε))$, where $N$ is the grid size and $ε$ is the error, for the discrete Charlier, Meixner and Krawtchouk transforms and for the integer-order Laguerre transform in the continuous setting. The efficient QOPTs are obtained by uncovering the links between Askey scheme polynomials and Gaussian quantum optical gates and developing a compilation framework for $SU(2)$ and $SU(1,1)$ optical gates on Cartesian grids extending the framework developed by Iyer et al. (arXiv:2602.15180). Further, we reduce the continuous Jacobi transform to the discrete Hahn transform and provide an $O\!\left(N\operatorname{polylog}((N+α+β+1)/ε)\right)$-time Hahn transform, a quadratic speedup over the naive implementation. This is based on a more efficient compilation of the Clebsch--Gordan transform for coupling $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ representations with spins $(j_{1}, j_{2})$. Finally, we develop a new framework for Laguerre transforms for all orders $ν>0$ by fast-forwarding the corresponding radial oscillator using a 3-term chirp decomposition and an efficient algorithm for the Quantum Hankel Transform on a logarithmic grid.

</details>


### [95] [Robustness of quantum spectrum estimation: weak Schur sampling under noisy inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03582)
*Vladyslav Visnevskyi,Laura Mančinska*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次研究含噪输入下的弱Schur采样（WSS），证明在每份副本与目标态ε接近的乘积态噪声模型下，测量输出误差不会累积为nε，而至多为ε+η(n,d)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WSS是量子谱估计（QSE）与量子态层析（QST）等最优量子学习算法的核心测量手段，但标准分析假设n份输入副本完全相同，而实际噪声场景下副本并不相同，破坏了WSS赖以成立的置换对称性。因此需要研究WSS在噪声输入下的鲁棒性。

Method: 在最简单的噪声模型中，副本相互独立但不完全相同（构成乘积态），每份与d维目标态ρ的迹距离在ε以内；进一步对任意输入ω，证明WSS的结果可观测量关于De Palma等人的量子Wasserstein距离W₁是1/n-Lipschitz的。

Result: 证明总的变差输出误差至多为ε+η(n,d)，其中η(n,d)=O(d/√n)为无噪误差，加性噪声项ε是最优的；对任意输入ω，误差至多为η(n,d)+(1/n)‖ω-ρ^{⊗n}‖_{W₁}。该结果首次给出Keyl和Werner的QSE算法在提出二十余年后的噪声鲁棒性证明。同时表明在该鲁棒性模型下，承诺条件不能弱化为单副本边缘接近或仅全局迹距离预算，因为这两类情况下都存在噪声输入击败所有测量与估计器。

Conclusion: 本文为实际场景中最优量子学习迈出必要一步，并推动量子信息论中诸多其他问题的鲁棒性研究。

Abstract: We initiate the study of weak Schur sampling (WSS) under noisy input. Many optimal quantum learning algorithms rely on this measurement, for instance in such fundamental tasks as quantum spectrum estimation (QSE) and quantum state tomography (QST). The standard analysis of WSS, QSE and QST assumes that the $n$ input copies of the target state $ρ$ are identical. We study what happens when they are not, a more realistic noisy scenario that breaks the very permutation symmetry on which WSS is built. In the simplest noise model, the copies are independent but not necessarily identical, i.e. they form a product state, and each is $ε$-close in trace distance to the $d$-dimensional target state $ρ$. A naive data-processing argument lets the per-copy errors accumulate to $nε$ on measurement output, growing with the input size. We prove that they do not accumulate: the expected output error in total variation is at most $ε+η(n,d)$, where $η(n,d)=O(d/\sqrt n)$ is the noiseless error, and the additive noise term $ε$ is optimal. Beyond noisy product states, for arbitrary inputs $ω$ we show that the outcome observables of WSS are $1/n$-Lipschitz with respect to the quantum Wasserstein distance $W_1$ of De Palma et al., so the error is at most $η(n,d)+\frac{1}{n}\|ω-ρ^{\otimes n}\|_{W_1}$. In particular, our results imply, for the first time, noise-robustness of Keyl and Werner's seminal algorithm for QSE, more than two decades after its introduction. Within our model of robustness, the promise cannot be weakened to closeness of single-copy marginals or to a global trace-distance budget alone, since in both cases some noisy inputs defeat every measurement and estimator. Our results are a necessary step towards optimal quantum learning in practice, and help make progress towards robustness of many other problems in quantum information theory.

</details>


### [96] [A Complete Proof of $\mathsf{QMA}_1$-Hardness for Weighted Gapped Clique Homology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03584)
*Tal Barak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文完整证明了带权、谱带隙版本的团同调问题是 $\mathsf{QMA}_1(\mathcal G)$-难的，修补并重建了 King-Kohler 归约中局部拓扑与谱性质的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kaibel 与 Pfetsch 提出单纯同调的计算复杂度问题，Crichigno 与 Kohler 证明团同调为 $\mathsf{QMA}_1$-难；King 与 Kohler 随后引入带权、谱带隙版本并给出归约，欲建立同样硬度结论，但其论证对非平凡折叠局部器件的分析存在缺口。本文旨在补全该困难方向的证明。

Method: 对归约实际产生的团复形重新建立所需的拓扑与谱性质：用精确有限证书刻画局部组合数据，结合相对同调确定各局部器件对编码量子比特空间的作用；证明所需的有限维滤波 Hodge 陈述，以恢复对应的秩一低能罚项；并推导定量整体估计，将源哈密顿量的保证间隙传递到最终的带权 Hodge 拉普拉斯算子。分析涉及局部拓扑、滤波上链复形以及大量重叠局部器件的相互作用。

Result: 对固定、可高效计算的 $k(n)$ 与逆多项式 $\gamma(n)$，带权带隙团同调是 $\mathsf{QMA}_1(\mathcal G)$-难的；已有 $\mathsf{QMA}$ 包含性论证在逻辑上独立于间隙，不受影响。

Conclusion: 该工作并非对已发表证明的局部修补，而是通过重建局部拓扑、滤波上链复形与重叠器件相互作用分析，完整给出困难方向证明，从而确立带权带隙团同调的 $\mathsf{QMA}_1(\mathcal G)$ 硬度。

Abstract: The computational complexity of simplicial homology has been studied since the question was raised by Kaibel and Pfetsch, and clique homology was later shown to be $\mathsf{QMA}_1$-hard by Crichigno and Kohler. King and Kohler subsequently introduced the weighted, spectrally gapped version considered here and developed a reduction intended to establish the same hardness result in this setting.
  We give a complete proof of the hard direction. The earlier King-Kohler argument contains a gap in the analysis of the nontrivially folded local gadgets, so the required topological and spectral properties have to be re-established on the clique complexes actually produced by the reduction. This is not a local repair of the published proof: the argument requires a new analysis of the local topology, the filtered cochain complexes, and the interaction of many overlapping gadgets.
  Our proof uses exact finite certificates for the local combinatorial data and relative homology to determine the effect of each gadget on the encoded qubit space. We then prove the finite-dimensional filtered-Hodge statement needed to recover the corresponding rank-one low-energy penalty and derive a quantitative global estimate that transfers the source-Hamiltonian promise gap to the final weighted Hodge Laplacian. Consequently, for fixed efficiently computable $k(n)$ and inverse-polynomial $γ(n)$, weighted gapped clique homology is $\mathsf{QMA}_1(\mathcal G)$-hard. The previously known $\mathsf{QMA}$-containment argument is logically independent of the gap and remains unaffected.

</details>


### [97] [Lifting Multiplicity in Randomized Benchmarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03586)
*Yale Fan,J. P. Marceaux*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出"提升随机基准测试"(lifted randomized benchmarking)，通过将目标群相对于扩大的群进行基准测试，从根本上消除多重性导致的多指数拟合问题，同时保持对态制备与测量误差的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机基准测试(RB)是评估量子处理器操作保真度的标准技术，但当群由原生门自然构成时往往存在多重性(multiplicity)，即过程矩阵表示不唯一，导致衰减曲线为多指数叠加，难以从数据中得到可靠结论。

Method: 提出提升随机基准测试方法：将character RB与synthetic RB的关键要素加以推广，把待基准测试的目标门群相对于一个扩大的群(lifted group)进行基准测试，从而内在性地解决多重性问题，完全无需拟合多指数衰减，同时仍对态制备与测量(SPAM)误差保持鲁棒。

Result: 数值实验表明，该方法能够高效且可靠地估计与实验离子阱系统相关的双量子比特门保真度。

Conclusion: 该技术可推广至随机基准测试之外，应用于量子层析、量子学习以及纠错码的表征等领域。

Abstract: Randomized benchmarking comprises a suite of standard techniques for assessing the operational fidelity of quantum processors. These techniques can be reliably applied to groups with process matrix representations that are free of multiplicity. However, many groups that are naturally formed from native gates do have multiplicity, and it is difficult to draw robust conclusions from the resulting data. In this work, we present a new and intrinsic solution to the problem of multiplicity that completely eliminates the need to fit multi-exponential decays while still remaining robust to errors in state preparation and measurement. Our procedure, which we call lifted randomized benchmarking, generalizes key elements of both character and synthetic randomized benchmarking to benchmark a target group of quantum gates with respect to an enlarged group. We illustrate in numerical examples that our method can efficiently and reliably estimate the fidelity of two-qubit gates relevant to experimental trapped-ion systems. These techniques may find applications beyond randomized benchmarking to quantum tomography and learning, as well as the characterization of error-correcting codes.

</details>


### [98] [Quantum Fire with Delegated Cloning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03593)
*Rohit Chatterjee,Ananta Mukherjee,Vir Pathak,Supartha Podder*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"可委托量子火"(delegatable quantum fire)原语,将克隆能力与公开操作分离,需独立密钥才能克隆。作者在经典预言机模型下给出两种构造:一种基于经典秘密钥,另一种称为torch-fire,使用量子克隆钥(torch),满足"m把torch最多使m个非通信方各克隆一个新火焰"的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有量子火(quantum fire)的克隆是公开操作,任何持有火焰状态者均可克隆,无法实现访问控制。为支持签发者仅授权指定量子服务器克隆、而剥夺其他持有者克隆能力的需求,需引入需要独立密钥的克隆机制。

Method: 在经典预言机模型(允许量子叠加查询公开经典预言机)下构造两种方案:(1)使用经典秘密钥控制克隆,持有完整密钥者方可成功克隆;(2)torch-fire使用量子克隆钥torch,克隆时torch自身不受影响,且不可被拆分或委托以产生额外克隆能力。安全性刻画为:给定m个torch的高效敌手,在可先联合处理资源、再任意分配纠缠寄存器的情况下,至多使m个非通信方各自克隆一个新的、独立签发的挑战火焰。

Result: 给出两种经典预言机模型下的可委托量子火构造。第一种基于经典秘密钥实现克隆授权;第二种torch-fire实现量子钥克隆,具备克隆钥不可拆分、不可委托的性质,并达到"m个torch不超过m次有效克隆"的抗合谋安全性。

Conclusion: 可委托量子火通过引入独立克隆密钥,将克隆从公开操作转变为受控操作,适用于访问控制场景;torch-fire进一步以量子钥形式保证克隆能力的不可复制与不可委托,两种构造均可在支持量子叠加查询的公开经典预言机模型下实现。

Abstract: Quantum fire is a recently introduced cryptographic primitive consisting of efficiently preparable quantum states, called \emph{flames}, that admit efficient cloning but resist efficient telegraphing, namely reconstruction via classical communication without preshared entanglement. In all prior constructions of quantum fire, cloning is a public operation that requires no separate key and every holder of a flame state can clone it. For applications to access control, however, an issuer may wish to delegate cloning to designated quantum servers while withholding this capability from other flame holders. To address this, we introduce \emph{delegatable quantum fire}, in which cloning requires a separate key.
  We give two constructions in the classical-oracle model. Our first construction uses a classical secret key which enables cloning, and any user with the entire key may clone successfully. Our second construction, which we call \emph{torch-fire}, uses quantum cloning keys, called \emph{torches}, which enable cloning while remaining unaffected in the process but cannot otherwise be split or delegated to enable additional cloning. An efficient adversary given $m$ torches cannot, except with negligible probability, enable more than $m$ noncommunicating parties to each clone a fresh, independently issued challenge flame. The adversary may jointly process its resources before separating the parties and distribute arbitrarily entangled registers among them. Both constructions are in the oracle model, relying on public classical oracles that allow queries in quantum superposition.

</details>


### [99] [Fault-tolerant and fully addressable unitary logical gates via round-robin sparsification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03594)
*Kaavya Sahay,Cody Jones*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出显式低深度酉电路，实现了部分二维平移不变qLDPC码的全可寻址、容错双量子比特Clifford门。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qLDPC码具有高效编码能力，但只有实现对单个逻辑信息单元的可寻址操作（addressable gates），才能最大化其编码效用。现有方案在可寻址性与容错性、电路深度方面存在不足，因此需要构造兼具低深度、全可寻址和容错特性的逻辑门方案。

Method: 作者通过对round-robin电路施加几何结构化的稀疏化（sparsification）程序，构造横向（transversal）酉电路，从而实现对二维平移不变qLDPC码子集的全可寻址、容错双量子比特Clifford门。并在toric、cyclic hypergraph-product、color、gross及directional等码上提出并数值模拟了一系列可寻址CZ门。

Result: 数值模拟表明，所提出的可寻址CZ门方案在多种码上均表现出类似存储器的逻辑错误率标度（fault-tolerant memory-like logical error rate scaling），验证了协议的普遍适用性。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了构造低深度、全可寻址、容错双量子比特Clifford门的一般性框架，适用于多类二维平移不变qLDPC码，为充分发挥qLDPC码高效编码优势提供了可行途径。

Abstract: For quantum low-density parity check (qLDPC) codes, the ability to manipulate individual logical units of information, i.e. the use of addressable gates, enables maximal utility of their efficient encoding. We introduce explicit low-depth unitary circuits that realize fully addressable and fault-tolerant two-qubit Clifford gates for a subset of two-dimensional translationally invariant qLDPC codes. Our transversal unitaries are created by applying a geometrically structured sparsification procedure to round-robin circuits. We verify the general applicability of our protocol by proposing and numerically simulating a range of addressable CZs between toric, cyclic hypergraph-product, color, gross, and directional codes, demonstrating fault-tolerant memory-like logical error rate scaling.

</details>


### [100] [The exact LCU sampling overhead of collective diagonal unitaries: resonances and a continued-fraction dichotomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03595)
*Zhihui Wang,Sujit Roy,Manil Maskey,Rahul Ramachandran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定实现二次集体相位 $e^{-i\gamma K^2}$ 的最小LCU开销 $\Gamma$ ，其增长由 $\gamma/\pi$ 的连分数决定：有理角 $\pi p/q$ 代价恰为 $q$，代价为 $\Theta(n)$ 当且仅当 $\gamma/\pi$ 坏可逼近；非对称情形可指数增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二次集体相位是约束量子优化中的基数惩罚层、自旋压缩的单轴扭转门以及玻色模的Kerr相位，但其实现成本此前缺乏精确刻画。作者希望确定用单量子比特旋转层线性组合（LCU）采样该门所需的最小开销 $\Gamma$，并回答最小编译代价的结构性来源。

Method: 将二次相位不作为两比特门，而采样为单量子比特旋转层的线性组合；以 $\gamma/\pi$ 的连分数为核心工具分析最小开销。对置换对称 $K$ 给出下界并证明任意单比特门不能改进；对二进制权重 $K=\sum_j 2^j x_j$ 引入新的“证书提升”（certificate-lifting）论证给出指数下界；并与 Fourier 型 LCU、Kerr 光学分数复兴分解等已有构造比较最优性。

Result: 无辅助比特采样以因子 $\Gamma$ 复现目标输出概率，且不存在对所有输入都更小的因子；置换对称时任意单比特门不更优。有理角 $\pi p/q$（最简）对所有 $n\ge2q-2$ 代价恰为 $\Gamma=q$；代价为 $\Theta(n)$ 当且仅当 $\gamma/\pi$ 坏可逼近；给出近低分母有理数两侧成立的主公式。非对称情形下二进制权重 $K$ 的平方相位在 $m$ 比特上至少需 $2^{0.557m-O(1)}$，超出所有割界 $2^{m/2}$。Fourier 型 LCU 的 $\Gamma\le n+1$ 在固定 $n$ 典型角下最优至常数因子；有理角下唯一最优是 Kerr 光学的分数复兴分解，且与 $n$ 无关。玻色模拟中 Kerr 门相移分解是相移器上的唯一最优，有限代价仅存在于有理 $\gamma/\pi$。

Conclusion: 二次集体相位的最小LCU开销由 $\gamma/\pi$ 的丢番图性质精确控制：连分数分母给出有理角代价，坏可逼近性对应线性于 $n$ 的代价，非对称情形呈现指数级下界。既有Fourier与Kerr构造在相应设定下达到最优，分数复兴分解是独立于寄存器规模的有理角唯一最优方案。

Abstract: We consider the cost of implementing the quadratic collective phase $e^{-iγK^2}$, with $K$ a collective observable of evenly spaced spectrum, such as the permutation-symmetric Hamming weight. This gate is the cardinality-penalty layer of constrained quantum optimization, the one-axis-twisting gate of spin squeezing and the Kerr phase of a bosonic mode. Instead of two-qubit gates, it can be sampled as a linear combination of single-qubit rotation layers (an LCU) at an overhead $Γ$. We determine the minimal overhead. Ancilla-free sampling of the layers reproduces the target's outcome probabilities up to this factor, and no smaller factor works for every input; when $K$ is permutation symmetric, arbitrary single-qubit gates do no better. The minimal overhead's growth with the register size $n$ is set by the continued fraction of $γ/π$: rational angles $πp/q$ in lowest terms cost exactly $Γ=q$ for every $n\ge2q-2$; the cost is $Θ(n)$ if and only if $γ/π$ is badly approximable; and a master formula, proved two-sided near low-denominator rationals, reads it off the nearest one. Without the symmetry the cost over single-qubit gates can be exponential: for the binary-weighted $K=\sum_j2^jx_j$ the squaring phase of Fourier arithmetic on $m$ qubits costs at least $2^{0.557m-O(1)}$, beyond the $2^{m/2}$ ceiling of every cut bound, by a new certificate-lifting argument. The Fourier-based LCU of Carrera Vazquez, Egger and Woerner (2026), which guarantees $Γ\le n+1$, is optimal up to a constant factor at typical angles at fixed $n$; at rational angles the unique optimizer is the fractional-revival decomposition of Kerr optics, a recipe independent of $n$. In bosonic simulation, the Kerr-gate phase-shifter decomposition of Upreti, Quesada and Chabaud (2026) is the unique optimum over phase shifters, and a finite-cost one exists only at rational $γ/π$.

</details>


### [101] [Efficient fidelity simulation of high-rate magic distillation circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03605)
*Xiao Xiao,Dominik Hangleiter,J. Pablo Bonilla Ataides,Rohan Mehta,Varun Menon,Mikhail D. Lukin,Michael J. Gullans*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出高效精确的经典方法，用于基准测试由X、CNOT及Clifford层级对角门组成的容错电路，避免模拟逻辑测量，复杂度对物理比特数n和电路深度T呈多项式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非Clifford门是超越Gottesman-Knill定理、实现有用容错量子算法的关键。但此类电路的容错性能难以准确评估，因为经典模拟这些电路在计算上是困难的，尤其涉及逻辑测量。

Method: 避开对逻辑测量的模拟，针对由X、CNOT和Clifford层级对角门（含带前馈的态传送与门传送gadget）组成的电路，开发精确且高效的经典基准测试方法。对含第三级对角门的电路，算法高效采样综合征统计量，估计输出逻辑保真度及特定Clifford可观测量（含所有Pauli可观测量）的期望值；综合征期望值评估可扩展至第四级对角门电路。算法在物理量子比特数n和电路深度T上为多项式复杂度，且与逻辑量子比特数无关。

Result: 实现对综合征统计量的高效采样，并估计输出逻辑保真度及Clifford可观测量期望值；期望值评估可扩展至第四级对角门。应用包括IQP采样与magic态制备/培养，并用于优化基于三个[[27,3,3]] tricycle码副本的高速率magic态蒸馏工厂。

Conclusion: 该经典模拟算法在容错基准测试中具有实用价值，可有效优化magic态蒸馏工厂等容错量子计算组件。

Abstract: Non-Clifford gates are essential for avoiding classical simulability under the Gottesman-Knill theorem. An error-corrected circuit with non-Clifford gates will therefore be an important element for realizing useful fault-tolerant quantum algorithms. However, accurately understanding the fault-tolerant performance of such circuits faces challenges due to the classical hardness of simulating these circuits. We avoid simulating logical measurements and develop exact and efficient classical methods for benchmarking circuits composed of X, CNOT, and diagonal gates in the Clifford hierarchy, including state and gate teleportation gadgets with feedforward. For circuits with third-level diagonal gates, our benchmarking algorithm efficiently samples syndrome statistics and estimates the output logical fidelity and expectation values of certain Clifford observables, including all Pauli observables. The evaluation of syndrome expectation values also extends to circuits with fourth-level diagonal gates. Our algorithm scales polynomially in the number of physical qubits n and circuit depth T, independent of the number of logical qubits. The benchmarking applications include IQP sampling and magic state preparation/cultivation. We demonstrate the practical utility of our simulation algorithms by using them to optimize high-rate magic state distillation factories based on three copies of a [[27,3,3]] tricycle code.

</details>


### [102] [The QICK Box: A Modular RF Front-End System for Quantum Control and Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03609)
*L. Arnaldi,D. Martin,S. Sussman,S. Uemura,K. Treptow,N. Wilcer,A. Colón Cesaní,O. Seidel,K. Anyang,G. Bratrud,F. Abouzahr,J. Christiansen-Salameh,D. Baxter,G. Cancelo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: QICK Box 是一款开源量子控制系统的硬件平台，具备最多16路输出、8路输入和8路20位直流偏置，支持快速直流耦合与高达10 GHz的射频信号链，并在六比特超导transmon器件上实现(91.7±0.4)%的单次读出保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QICK自2022年推出后需向新硬件平台迁移并拓展功能，以适配超导、原子、离子阱、自旋缺陷等多种量子比特以及量子传感器和量子网络应用，因此需要详细阐述其硬件性能以支撑高保真量子控制与测量。

Method: 介绍QICK Box的硬件细节与性能：最多16路输出、8路输入、8路20位直流偏置；采用模块化输入输出子卡，可选择针对快速直流耦合信号或最高10 GHz射频信号优化的信号链；功率水平、步进衰减和带通滤波均由软件控制。通过Fermilab QUIET测试台上六transmon器件的单次读出测量来验证性能。

Result: 实现(91.7±0.4)%的读出保真度，该保真度受读出过程中T1衰减限制；在未使用量子放大器的情况下，单次读出信噪比(SNR)为4.4±0.1。

Conclusion: QICK Box适用于高保真量子控制与测量应用；其固件与软件方面的进展将在另一篇论文中描述。

Abstract: The QICK (Quantum Instrumentation Control Kit), first introduced in 2022, is a standalone open source control system for quantum information science. Since then, QICK has been migrated to new hardware platforms, expanded its functionality for applications with different types of qubits (superconducting, atomic, ion-trapped, and spin defects), as well as quantum sensors, and quantum networks. QICK is a highly optimized control kit, with a library of functions that can be used for more than one application. In particular, the software and firmware are customized to minimize latency and maximize control performance and throughput. In this white paper, we describe hardware details and performance of the QICK Box. The QICK Box features up to 16 outputs, up to 8 inputs, and 8 20-bit DC biases. Modular output and input daughtercards give the choice of signal chains optimized for fast DC-coupled signals or RF signals up to 10 GHz. Power levels, step attenuation, and bandpass filtering are controlled by software. We demonstrate the system's performance through single-shot readout measurements of a six-transmon device at the Quantum Underground Instrumentation Experimental Testbed (QUIET) at Fermilab, achieving (91.7 +/- 0.4)% readout fidelity limited by T1 decay during the readout. The single shot readout signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) is 4.4 +/- 0.1 without a quantum amplifier, confirming that the QICK Box is suitable for high-fidelity quantum control and measurement applications. Advances in firmware and software will be described in a separate paper.

</details>


### [103] [Symmetry-preserving quantum compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03624)
*Maryam Mudassar,Zhi-Yuan Wei,Alexander Schuckert,Michael J. Lawler,Michael J. Gullans,Daniel Gottesman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出精确保持SU(2)对称性的容错编译框架，利用测量与前馈绕过数论障碍，使编译误差限制在对称算子代数内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Clifford+T综合即使目标演化保持连续对称性，也可能破坏该对称性，导致编译后的量子模拟丢失原系统关键特征。

Method: 刻画阻止保持对称性的Clifford+T幺正电路逼近任意SU(2)对称演化的数论障碍，并通过测量与前馈加以规避，构建保持对称性的容错编译框架；进一步推广到格点规范理论的规范对称性以及量子化学与Fermi-Hubbard模型中相关的费米子粒子数与自旋对称性。

Result: 在一维Heisenberg模型输运模拟中，相同匹配T-count（每两格点Trotter步54个T门）下，标准综合给出错误的输运指数，而所提方法复现预期指数；在达到同等指数估计精度时，T-count约降低5倍。

Conclusion: 保持对称性的容错编译可定性改变综合误差的影响，并显著降低量子模拟所需资源。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum simulation requires compiling symmetry-preserving time evolutions into discrete gate sets. Standard Clifford+T synthesis can break continuous symmetries even when the target evolution preserves them, causing compiled simulations to lose essential features of the original system. We develop a fault-tolerant compilation framework that preserves SU(2) symmetry exactly. We characterize the number-theoretic obstruction preventing symmetry-preserving unitary Clifford+T circuits from approximating arbitrary SU(2)-symmetric evolutions and circumvent it using measurement and feed-forward. The resulting synthesis confines compilation errors to the symmetry-preserving operator algebra rather than introducing symmetry-breaking perturbations.
  We demonstrate the algorithm by simulating transport in the one-dimensional Heisenberg model. At a lower matched T-count, for instance, 54 T gates per two-site Trotter step, standard synthesis yields an incorrect transport exponent, whereas our symmetry-preserving compilation reproduces the expected exponent for the same expected T-count. If instead each approach is allocated whatever resources are necessary to estimate this exponent to the same accuracy, our construction reduces the T-count by approximately a factor of five. We further extend the framework to gauge symmetries in lattice gauge theories and to particle-number and spin symmetries in fermionic models relevant to quantum chemistry and the Fermi-Hubbard model.
  These results show that symmetry-preserving fault-tolerant compilation can qualitatively change the effects of synthesis error while substantially reducing the resources required for quantum simulation.

</details>


### [104] [Discriminating Lindbladian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03629)
*Robert Salzmann,Ludovico Lami,Martin B. Plenio,Susana F. Huelga*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由Lindbladian生成的量子马尔可夫动力学辨识（量子假设检验）的基本极限，关注总暴露时间与时间分辨率的角色，通过量子Zeno协议将时间分辨率确立为一种辨识资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子假设检验此前主要针对量子态与量子信道展开研究，而由Lindbladian生成的量子马尔可夫动力学（连续时间演化）的辨识极限尚不明确。本文旨在刻画区分任意一对此类动力学的根本极限，并厘清总暴露时间与时间分辨率在其中所扮演的角色。

Method: 允许自适应地选择演化时长，并允许在时长之间施加任意瞬时控制。将最优II型误差指数（Stein指数）刻画为量子相对熵的最大瞬时增长率，并基于Lindbladian给出其有限性的代数判据。借助量子Zeno协议实现上述极限的达成。

Result: Stein指数等于量子相对熵的最大瞬时增量；无限指数等价于在足够大的有限总时间下、对任意正I型容忍度，II型误差概率均可消失。部分动力学存在零II型误差的有限协议，另一些则需要越来越精细的时间控制：后者误差随最小允许演化时长（分辨率时间）趋于零而消失，但在任意固定正分辨率时间下始终有界远离零。当零假设为单位半群时，无限渐近误差指数意味着在固定有限总暴露时间下可使II型误差任意小且I型误差严格为零，这在对应经典情形中不可能实现。以量子比特上区分单位半群与广义振幅阻尼为例，展示了三种情形。

Conclusion: 总暴露时间与时间分辨率是区分量子Lindbladian动力学的关键资源。时间分辨率可作为一种辨识资源：某些动力学虽无有限零误差协议，但可通过量子Zeno协议在分辨率时间趋于零时使II型误差消失。无限误差指数在单位半群零假设下带来比经典情形更强的推论，量子比特模型的具体实例验证了三种 regime 的存在。

Abstract: The fundamental task of quantum hypothesis testing, which consists in discriminating quantum objects, has been extensively studied for quantum states and channels. Here, we investigate the fundamental limits of discriminating an arbitrary pair of quantum Markovian dynamics generated by Lindbladians, focusing on the roles of total exposure time and temporal resolution. We allow adaptive choices of evolution durations and arbitrary instantaneous controls between them. We characterise the optimal type-II error exponent (Stein exponent) as the largest instantaneous increase of quantum relative entropy, and give an algebraic criterion for its finiteness in terms of the Lindbladians. Remarkably, an infinite exponent is equivalent to a vanishing type-II error probability at sufficiently large finite total time, for every positive type-I tolerance. While some dynamics admit a finite protocol with zero type-II error, others require increasingly fine temporal control. For the latter, the error vanishes as the minimum allowed evolution duration, or resolution time, tends to zero at fixed sufficiently large total time, but remains bounded away from zero at every fixed positive resolution time. We achieve this using quantum Zeno protocols, establishing temporal resolution as a resource for discrimination. When the null hypothesis is the identity semigroup, an infinite asymptotic error exponent has a stronger consequence: at a fixed finite total exposure time, the type-II error can be made arbitrarily small while keeping the type-I error exactly zero. This is impossible in the corresponding classical setting. Discriminating the identity semigroup from generalised amplitude damping on a qubit provides examples of all three regimes: a finite asymptotic error exponent, zero type-II error attainable at positive resolution time, and vanishing type-II error requiring the resolution time to tend to zero.

</details>


### [105] [Entanglement of purification for Werner states: canonical purification and nonadditivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03630)
*Zahra Baghali Khanian,Amir-Reza Negari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了von Neumann纠缠纯化（entanglement of purification）的非可加性：对singlet权重在 $1/64\le f\le1/4$ 区间的两量子比特Werner态 $W(f)$，其规范纯化（canonical purification）全局最优，纯化纠缠等于反射熵；而当 $0<f<f_\ast$（$f_\ast\approx0.017$）时，正则化纯化纠缠严格小于反射熵，从而在 $1/64\le f<f_\ast$ 上确立非可加性，解决了 $\alpha=1$ 时的可加性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠度量的可加性/nonadditivity是量子信息理论的核心基础问题。Rényi纯化纠缠在 $0\le\alpha<1$ 已被证明非可加，但 $\alpha=1$（即von Neumann情形）的可加性长期悬而未决。本文旨在填补 $\alpha=1$ 这一关键空白，并探究非可加性的来源。

Method: 以两量子比特Werner态 $W(f)$（singlet权重 $1/64\le f\le1/4$）为研究对象，证明其规范纯化的全局最优性（推广了文献[1]中基于经典两量子比特态、用于 $0\le\alpha<1$ Rényi非可加的证明思路），从而给出纯化纠缠的精确值（即反射熵），并比较正则化纯化纠缠与反射熵的大小关系。

Result: 证明von Neumann纯化纠缠非可加。对 $1/64\le f\le1/4$，规范纯化全局最优，其纠缠（反射熵）恰为纯化纠缠；对 $0<f<f_\ast$（$f_\ast\approx0.017$），正则化纯化纠缠严格小于反射熵。两者合并在 $1/64\le f<f_\ast$ 上确立非可加性。值得注意的是，非可加性再次由可分态见证，底层态无需含有纠缠。

Conclusion: 本文解决了纯化纠缠在 $\alpha=1$ 处的可加性问题，确认von Neumann纠缠纯化非可加，并表明非可加性无需底层态的纠缠即可被可分态见证，深化了对纠缠度量可加性结构的理解。

Abstract: We prove that the von Neumann entanglement of purification is nonadditive. For two-qubit Werner states $W(f)$ with singlet weight $1/64\le f\le1/4$, we show that canonical purification is globally optimal: its entanglement, also known as reflected entropy, gives the exact entanglement of purification. In contrast, the regularized entanglement of purification lies strictly below the reflected entropy for $0<f<f_\ast$, where $f_\ast\approx0.017$. Together, these results establish nonadditivity throughout $1/64\le f<f_\ast$. Our canonical-optimality proof generalizes the proof in [1], which established Rényi nonadditivity for $0\leα<1$ using classical two-qubit states. We thus resolve the additivity question at $α=1$. Remarkably, nonadditivity is again witnessed by separable states and no entanglement in the underlying states is required.

</details>


### [106] [Polynomial-Time Algorithms for Nuclear Tensor Norms and Multipartite Separability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03637)
*Martino Bernasconi,Giulio Malavolta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有界Frobenius范数张量的多线性优化问题，其中k个维度为d的局部因子各自取自凸集；给出常数加性近似下运行时间为d^{O(k)}的确定性算法，并首次实现高阶张量核范数单位球弱成员判定与多体量子可分离性（Frobenius范数）的常数精度多项式时间算法，同时给出基于量子副本的量子实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有界Frobenius范数张量的多线性优化在计算复杂性与量子信息中具有核心地位，但既有方法难以在常数精度下高效求解；高阶张量核范数单位球的弱成员判定与多体量子可分离性检验长期缺乏多项式时间算法，因此需要新的算法视角与工具。

Method: 将张量优化建模为合作型多证明者博弈，并结合递归谱压缩（递归谱压缩过程）设计确定性算法；随后把相关思想量子化，在输入态以副本形式给出（而非显式经典描述）时构造量子算法，用于Frobenius范数可分离性检验。

Result: 得到常数加性近似下运行时间为d^{O(k)}的确定性算法；据此首次获得高阶张量核范数单位球弱成员判定与多体量子可分离性（Frobenius范数）的常数精度多项式时间算法；在量子副本设定下，实现使用poly(k)个副本、poly(k log d)时间的Frobenius范数可分离性检验算法。

Conclusion: 多证明者博弈视角与递归谱压缩为张量多线性优化提供了有效框架，并可推广至量子设定；该工作首次在常数精度下打通了高阶张量核范数与多体量子可分离性的多项式时间判定，并为基于副本的量子可分离性检验给出高效算法。

Abstract: We study a multilinear optimization problem for tensors with bounded Frobenius norm, where each of the $k$ local factors of dimension $d$ is chosen from a convex set. We give a deterministic algorithm running in time $d^{O(k)}$ for constant additive approximations. As applications, we obtain the first polynomial-time algorithms at constant accuracy for weak membership in the nuclear-norm unit ball of high-order tensors and for multipartite quantum separability in Frobenius norm. Our approach views tensor optimization as a cooperative multiprover game and combines this perspective with a recursive spectral compression procedure. We then show that related ideas admit a quantum implementation when the input state is given through copies, rather than an explicit classical description. In these settings we obtain an algorithm to solve Frobenius-norm separability testing using $\text{poly}(k)$ copies and $\text{poly}(k\log d)$ time.

</details>


### [107] [Clarifications on the Experimental Status of Real Quantum Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03644)
*Timothée Hoffreumon,Mischa P. Woods*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文回应Renou等人声称的可以证伪实数量子理论而不证伪量子理论的实验，论证其结论依赖不可实验检验的前提，远未达到Bell型证明水准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前已证明，若不连带证伪量子理论，就无法实验证伪实数量子理论。该结论与Renou等人在Nature发表的、声称通过双局域网络实验可以单独证伪实数量子理论的文章核心主张直接矛盾。本文旨在辨析若干试图挽救该实验结论的自然辩护思路。

Method: 概念与逻辑分析：先说明这些结果虽基于非局域性实验，却远未达到Bell型证明的严格程度；再论证该实验隐含了"独立制备必然表示为乘积态"这一不可实验检验的前提；最后讨论若干超出两方结果范围的关切。

Result: 指出Renou等人的实验结论无法在保持量子理论成立的同时严格证伪实数量子理论，其关键弱点在于所依赖的前提在实验上不可检验。

Conclusion: 该双局域网络实验不足以单独证伪实数量子理论，其证明强度低于Bell型非局域性论证；相关辩护尝试均无法恢复其原本主张的实验结论。

Abstract: In our previous paper on the matter, we proved that real quantum theory (RQT) cannot be experimentally falsified without the experiment falsifying quantum theory (QT) as well. Our result is in direct contradiction to the central claim of an article by Renou et al., published in Nature [600, 625--629 (2021)], reporting a bilocal network experiment that could falsify real quantum theory without falsifying quantum theory.
  The purpose of this note is to address several seemingly natural attempts to recover the experimental conclusion of Renou et al. We first explain how these results, although based on nonlocality experiments, are far from a Bell-like level of proof. Then we explain why the proposed experiment requires an experimentally untestable premise about independent preparations necessarily being represented as product states. Finally, we discuss some concerns that lie outside the scope of both results.

</details>


### [108] [Exponential quantum space advantage in random data streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03645)
*Adam Bouland,Matthew Ding,Siddhartha Jain*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在随机顺序流式模型下证明了两个无条件的量子空间优势：Yamakawa–Zhandry 码交集问题具有指数级量子空间优势；最优多项式交集（OPI）问题在低速率区具有可证明的量子空间优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子计算在流式模型中的空间优势，特别是随机顺序输入下是否仍能实现指数级量子空间优势，并寻求更广泛问题的可证明量子优势。

Method: 利用密度恢复划分（density-restoring partitions）结合凸势能函数给出下界；并使用流式版本的解码量子干涉（Decoded Quantum Interferometry）算法给出上界。

Result: 码交集问题在随机顺序流式模型下具有指数级量子空间优势；OPI 问题中，达到 1/2+Ω(√(d/n)) 的约束满足比例，经典需 Ω(n) 比特内存，而量子仅需 O(d log n) 量子比特。

Conclusion: 在随机顺序流式模型下建立了无条件的量子空间优势，并在低速率区（评估点数远大于次数）实现了可证明的、甚至指数级的量子空间优势。

Abstract: We show two unconditional quantum space advantages in the random-order streaming model. First, we show the Yamakawa--Zhandry Code Intersection problem admits exponential quantum advantage in the streaming model when its inputs are streamed in random order. This means quantum computers exhibit exponential space advantage even when simply receiving $(x,f(x))$ pairs for a uniformly random function $f$ in a uniformly random order. Our lower bound is shown using density-restoring partitions as in the work of Göös, Gur, Jain, and Li (STOC 2025) combined with a convex potential, similar to the work of Raz (J.ACM 2018) on parity learning and its generalization by Garg, Raz, and Tal (STOC 2018).
  Second, we use our framework to show quantum space advantage for the Optimal Polynomial Intersection (OPI) problem in certain regimes via a streaming version of the Decoded Quantum Interferometry algorithm (Nature 2025; arXiv:2510.10967). In particular, we show that for degree $d$ and $n$ evaluation points, attaining $1/2 + Ω(\sqrt{d/n})$ fraction of satisfied OPI constraints via streaming requires $Ω(n)$ classical bits of memory but only $O(d\log n)$ qubits. This yields provable quantum advantage in a "low-rate" regime when the number of evaluation points is much larger than the degree, with a space advantage that can be as large as exponential in certain parameter settings.

</details>


### [109] [Reaching the Limits of Ground-State Metrology with Many-Body Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03653)
*Erik L. André,Víctor Izquierdo,Ricard Puig,John Calsamiglia,Martí Perarnau-Llobet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究利用多体探针（短程与长程相互作用）在基态测量中逼近量子Fisher信息上限的物理条件，区分静态基态上限与动态Heisenberg上限，并给出可接近这些极限的具体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在N体哈密顿量基态测量中，何种物理条件可使量子Fisher信息F_θ达到两个基本极限（静态基态上限F_θ≲N²/Δ²与动态Heisenberg极限F_θ≲N²τ²），从而为设计逼近量子精密测量极限的多体基态传感器提供理论依据。

Method: 分析短程与长程（全对全）相互作用体系的量子Fisher信息行为；讨论静态极限饱和所需的谱隙条件，包括二阶量子临界点临界指数关系ν(d+z)=1及一阶相变的cat-like基态；研究动态极限与基态制备时间的关系，并采用局域绝热协议获得逆能隙最优轮廓；以横场Ising链、XXZ链、压缩哈密顿量和双模Bose-Hubbard模型为范例进行分析。

Result: 短程相互作用体系饱和静态极限需要谱隙随N闭合，二阶量子临界点给出通用条件ν(d+z)=1（在XXZ链中可任意接近），一阶相变cat-like基态提供替代途径；全对全相互作用模型即使在有能隙区域也能达到最优。动态极限与基态制备时间紧密相关，其逆能隙最优轮廓可通过局域绝热协议实现。识别出可逼近两种极限的具体多体探针系统。

Conclusion: 研究为设计运行于量子精度最终极限的（局域）多体基态传感器提供了理论基础，明确了短程/长程相互作用体系达到静态与动态测量极限的条件及可行模型。

Abstract: We investigate the physical requirements to reach the ultimate limits of ground-state metrology -- i.e., the estimation of an unknown parameter $θ$ of an $N$-body Hamiltonian via measurements on its ground state -- using many-body probes featuring either short-range or long-range interactions. We characterize the conditions to saturate two fundamental limits of the quantum Fisher information $F_θ$: the static ground-state bound $F_θ\lesssim N^2/Δ^2$, where $Δ$ is the spectral gap, and the dynamical Heisenberg limit $F_θ\lesssim N^2 τ^2$, where $τ$ is the total protocol duration. To saturate the static limit, we argue that short-range interacting systems require a spectral gap that closes with $N$. At second-order quantum critical points this leads to a universal condition on the critical exponents, $ν(d+z)=1$, which can be approached arbitrarily closely, as we show for the XXZ chain; cat-like ground states at first-order transitions provide an alternative route to static optimality. In contrast, we find that all-to-all interacting models can achieve optimality even in gapped regimes. Furthermore, reaching the dynamical bound is tightly related to the ground-state preparation time; we demonstrate that its optimal, inverse-gap profile can be obtained via local adiabatic protocols. By analyzing paradigmatic systems -- the transverse-field Ising chain, the XXZ chain, a squeezing Hamiltonian, and the two-mode Bose-Hubbard model -- we identify specific many-body probes capable of approaching both limits. Overall, our results provide a theoretical basis for the design of (local) many-body ground-state sensors operating at the ultimate limits of quantum precision.

</details>


### [110] [A Basis-Aware Approach to Quantum Sampling of the Fermi-Hubbard Ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03654)
*Adam Leicester,Zixu Wang,Jack Mandell,Yangyang Xu,Osama Muhammad Raisuddin,Haimeng Zhang,Jian Shi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于采样的Krylov量子对角化 (SKQD) 中，物理启发的单体积轨道旋转所构成的表示基对目标态可压缩性的影响，发现最优基随相互作用强度与跃迁各向异性系统变化，且基选择显著影响采样效率，硬件规模下还需联合设计表示、量子线路与经典重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的量子对角化通过将经典对角化限制在量子采样得到的组态空间来降低多体计算成本，但其效率依赖目标态在采样基下的紧凑表示程度。作者希望探究这种依赖性，以明确何种基表示能提升SKQD效率，并为可扩展的采样对角化提供设计依据。

Method: 针对半填充Fermi-Hubbard梯子模型的基于采样Krylov量子对角化 (SKQD)，采用物理启发的单体积轨道旋转，比较位置、动量、成键/反键以及leg-mode等多种表示基；开展态矢量SKQD计算分析基选择对采样效率的影响，并在含多达120个量子比特的硬件系统上进行验证。

Result: 最可压缩的基随相互作用强度与跃迁各向异性系统变化：弱相互作用区动量采样更优，而当主导能量尺度改变时，几何或相互作用适配的表示更具优势；在中等相互作用与各向同性跃迁附近，即便最优表示仍难以压缩。态矢量计算表明基选择强烈影响采样效率；硬件计算保持了该基依赖性的定性特征，同时随系统规模增大出现粒子数泄漏增加与重构敏感性增强。

Conclusion: 可扩展的基于采样对角化需要采样表示、量子线路与经典重构流程的协同设计 (co-design)。

Abstract: Sample-based quantum diagonalization can reduce the classical cost of many-body calculations by restricting diagonalization to configurations identified through quantum sampling, but its efficiency depends on how compactly the target state is represented in the sampled basis. We investigate this dependence for sample-based Krylov quantum diagonalization of the half-filled Fermi-Hubbard ladder using physically motivated one-body orbital rotations. Across position, momentum, bonding/antibonding, and leg-mode representations, the most compressible basis varies systematically with interaction strength and hopping anisotropy, with momentum sampling favored in the weakly interacting regime and geometry- or interaction-adapted representations becoming advantageous as the dominant energy scale changes. Near intermediate interaction and isotropic hopping, however, even the most favorable representation remains poorly compressible. Statevector SKQD calculations further show that basis choice strongly affects sampling efficiency. Hardware calculations on systems containing up to 120 qubits retain qualitative signatures of this basis dependence while revealing increasing particle-number leakage and reconstruction sensitivity with system size. These results show that scalable sample-based diagonalization requires co-design of the sampled representation, quantum circuit, and classical reconstruction procedure.

</details>


### [111] [Breaking the cubic barrier for the inverse-free Solovay-Kitaev algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03655)
*Adam Bouland,Victor Shyaka,Chenyi Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无逆门集合的高效量子编译问题，构造了Pauli门加无理旋转的编译算法（指数≤≈2.138），并将方法推广到一般无逆情形（指数≤2.988），首次给出超越信息论下界3的编译算法实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solovay-Kitaev算法可实现polylog(1/ε)时间的编译，但最初针对逆封闭门集。Bouland与Giurgicǎ-Tiron将结果推广到无逆门集，但其编译算法指数很高（量子比特情形超过8.62），而信息论上已知无逆指数可达3。因此自然要问：能否改进Bouland-Giurgicǎ-Tiron算法的指数？是否存在指数低于3、超越信息论论证的情形？

Method: 首先处理无逆编译问题的特例——Pauli门辅以量子比特的无理旋转，融合Kuperberg以及Sardharwalla、Cubitt、Harrow、Linden的算法思想，构造高效的"Pauli golf"编译流程，得到指数至多约2.138的算法。随后将上述思想推广到量子比特的一般无逆情形，引入一种新颖的酉修正近似"瞄准"（aiming）技术，给出指数至多2.988的编译算法。

Result: Pauli门加无理旋转的特例获得指数至多约2.138的高效编译算法；量子比特一般无逆情形获得指数至多2.988的编译算法。两者均低于信息论下界3，是超越信息论论证的两个量子编译算法实例。

Conclusion: 本文给出两个超越信息论论证的量子编译算法实例，表明无逆编译存在大量效率提升空间，并提出了可进一步改进指数的方向。

Abstract: The Solovay-Kitaev algorithm states that it is possible to efficiently compile unitaries to accuracy $ε$ in $\text{polylog}(1/ε)$ time using any universal gate set. While Solovay and Kitaev's initial work addressed inverse-closed gate sets, recently Bouland and Giurgicǎ-Tiron showed it is possible to efficiently compile arbitrary gate sets without inverses. However, the exponent of their compilation algorithm is high. For example the inverse-free exponent for a qubit is over $8.62$, while information theoretically it is known that an inverse-free exponent of $3$ is possible in any dimension by a result of Oszmaniec, Sawicki, and Horodecki. This leads to a natural question: can one improve the exponent in Bouland and Giurgicǎ-Tiron's algorithm? And are there any cases where the inverse-free exponent is below 3, thus surpassing existing information-theoretic arguments?
  To answer this question, we first show that a special case of the inverse-free compiling problem -- namely Pauli gates supplemented by an irrational rotation of a qubit - admits a highly efficient compilation algorithm with an exponent at most $\approx 2.138$.
  Our algorithm fuses ideas from prior algorithms of Kuperberg and Sardharwalla, Cubitt, Harrow and Linden to construct an efficient ``Pauli golf'' compilation routine. We then extend these ideas to the general inverse-free case for a qubit, where we give a compiling algorithm with exponent at most $2.988$. This result uses a novel form of approximate ``aiming'' of unitary corrections to improve the compilation efficiency. Our results give two examples of quantum compiling algorithms surpassing information-theoretic arguments, and demonstrate that there are many efficiency gains possible for inverse-free compilation.

</details>


### [112] [Super-Exponential Advantage of Squeezed Light in Phase Estimation under Discrete Phase Randomisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03657)
*Lorcan O. Conlon,V Vijendran,Aritra Das,Yu-Xin Wang,Erfan Abbasgholinejad,Anthony J. Brady,Jacob Bringewatt,Sean R. Muleady,Syed M. Assad,Alexey V. Gorshkov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在相位估计任务中，对每通道平均光子数受限情形，压缩真空态相比相干态（及相位协变信道可达态）在量子Fisher信息上展现出关于随机整数M的超指数分离，并给出非渐近估计性能差异及噪声影响的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计量学普遍认为在合理先验信息假设下，量子资源带来的最大优势是灵敏度的二次（Heisenberg）加速。本文质疑并突破这一常规认知，考察在存在大随机相位偏移（未知均匀整数k，相位2πk/M）叠加小待估相位的信道中，是否可能存在超越二次的量子优势。

Method: 构建实现相位2πk/M（k为均匀随机未知整数）加小未知相位的信道模型；在每通道平均光子数约束下，比较压缩真空态与相干态（及相位协变信道可达态）的量子Fisher信息(QFI)；进行非渐近估计分析，给出显式压缩真空协议；并分析噪声（固定光学损耗与热噪声）下QFI及估计精度，采用外差探测。

Result: 每通道平均光子数为1时，压缩真空态与相干态QFI之比按Ω[(M/√2e)^M]增长（超指数）。光子数为M/log M时，压缩真空QFI为Ω(M^{3/2}/√log M)，相干态至多exp[-M log log M+O(M)]。非渐近结果：光子数M/log M下，压缩真空协议用O(√(M log M))次信道达到精度O(1/M)，而相干态协议至少需exp[M log log M-O(M)]次。噪声下QFI比仍可超指数增长。固定损耗与热噪声下，取n̄=M^2/(log M)^{3/2}，压缩真空加外差探测用亚多项式次信道达O(1/M)精度，经典族协议需超多项式次。

Conclusion: 在每通道光子数受限时，压缩真空态相对相干态可实现QFI和估计精度的超指数优势，且该优势在噪声和光学损耗下依然保持，超越了传统量子计量中的二次加速认知。

Abstract: A paradigmatic task in quantum metrology is that of phase estimation. For this task it is commonly believed, under reasonable assumptions about prior information, that a quadratic advantage in sensitivity is the maximum possible advantage offered by the use of quantum resources. In this work, we consider a channel that implements a phase shift by an angle $2πk/M$, where $k$ is a uniformly random unknown integer, in addition to a small unknown phase shift of interest. We show that, subject to a constraint on the mean photon number per channel use, there is a super-exponential separation between the quantum Fisher information (QFI) attainable using a squeezed vacuum state and that attainable using coherent states. This advantage holds over any state obtained from a coherent state via phase covariant channels. Specifically, when the mean photon number per channel use is 1, the ratio of their QFI grows as $Ω[(M/\sqrt{2}e)^M]$. For a mean photon number per channel use of $M/\log(M)$, the squeezed vacuum QFI is $Ω(M^{3/2}/\sqrt{\log(M)})$, whereas for a coherent state it is at most $\exp[-M\log\log(M)+O(M)]$. We complement this QFI analysis with non-asymptotic estimation results. For a mean photon number per channel use of $M/\log M$, an explicit squeezed-vacuum protocol estimates the phase to accuracy $O(1/M)$ using $O(\sqrt{M\log M})$ channel uses, whereas every coherent state protocol requires at least $\exp[M\log\log M-O(M)]$ channel uses. We consider the effects of noise and show that the ratio of their QFIs can still grow super-exponentially in $M$. Finally, under fixed optical loss and thermal noise, choosing $\bar n=M^2/(\log M)^{3/2}$ allows squeezed vacuum with heterodyne detection to achieve accuracy $O(1/M)$ using sub-polynomially many channel uses, whereas every protocol in the specified classical family requires super-polynomially many.

</details>


### [113] [A note on tomography of states with low stabilizer rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03658)
*Arkopal Dutt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究稳定子秩为k的n量子比特量子态的层析，证明其稳定子范围ξ≤2^O(k)，从而给出poly(n,2^k,1/ε)时间的层析协议，可高效处理k=O(log n)的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稳定子秩刻画了量子态偏离稳定子态的程度，是量子计算与量子信息中的重要复杂度度量。然而此前对稳定子范围（stabilizer extent）的最佳上界仅为k^O(k)，导致对具有小稳定子秩态的层析协议效率受限，难以覆盖k=O(log n)这一自然且有意义的参数区间。因此需要更紧的稳定子范围上界以改进层析效率。

Method: 首先证明稳定子秩为k的n量子比特态|ψ⟩的稳定子范围满足ξ(|ψ⟩)=min Σ|c_i|≤2^O(k)，将此前k^O(k)的界改进为指数级收紧；随后利用已有的针对有界稳定子范围态的层析协议，构造出在trace distance ε内恢复|ψ⟩的层析算法。

Result: 给出了运行时间为poly(n,2^k,1/ε)的层析协议，可在trace distance ε意义下重建稳定子秩为k的态；结合新界ξ≤2^O(k)，该协议对k=O(log n)的态具有多项式时间效率，显著优于基于旧上界k^O(k)的间接结果。

Conclusion: 通过将稳定子范围的上界从k^O(k)改进为2^O(k)，本文揭示稳定子秩与稳定子范围之间的指数关系，并据此获得高效的量子态层析协议，使k=O(log n)的态可被多项式时间学习。

Abstract: In this note, we consider tomography of $n$-qubit quantum states $|ψ\rangle$ that are promised to have stabilizer rank $k$ i.e., admit a stabilizer decomposition of $|ψ\rangle = \sum_{i=1}^k c_i |φ_i\rangle$ where each $|φ_i\rangle$ is a stabilizer state. We give a tomography protocol for such states up to trace distance $\varepsilon > 0$ running in $\textsf{poly}(n,2^k,1/\varepsilon)$ time and thus can handle $k=O(\log n)$ efficiently. This follows from first showing that the stabilizer extent $ξ(|ψ\rangle)$, which is the minimal $\ell_1$ norm over the coefficients $\{c_i\}$ of any stabilizer decomposition of $|ψ\rangle$, can be bounded as $ξ(|ψ\rangle) \leq 2^{O(k)}$ and improving upon the previous best bound of $k^{O(k)}$. The protocol is then obtained by utilizing a tomography protocol for states with bounded stabilizer extent.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum Codeword Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03659)
*John Blue,Zachary E. Chin,Vlad S. Oros,Isaac L. Chuang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在弱相位信号（Qθ→0）下，将量子传感器寄存器耦合到持续量子记忆，可比仅具经典记忆的同资源接收机更可靠地识别未知经典码字，并在辛普莱克斯码上实现纠错与渐近零误差解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子接收机在弱相位信号下识别经典码字时，量子记忆相对经典记忆是否具有根本优势，并量化该优势所需的qubit预算与角度条件。

Method: 每个接收比特以相同Z旋转角θ作用于所有传感qubit；允许辅助qubit、任意控制酉与纠缠，总qubit预算为Q；比较持续量子记忆与逐比特经典记忆两类接收机；针对偶k的[n,k]二元辛普莱克斯码设计接收方案。

Result: 对n=2^k−1的辛普莱克斯码，取θ=2π/(k√(n+1))、Q=Θ((t+1)log n)，可精确纠正至多t≤(k−2)/4个传输错误；当t=o(√k)时，同资源但仅经典记忆的接收机平均成功概率趋于0。独立比特翻转概率p<1/16时，量子记忆接收机以Q=O(log n log log n)使解码错误趋于0，而经典记忆接收机平均成功概率消失。

Conclusion: 持续量子记忆在弱信号、受限qubit预算下提供可证明的解码优势，可在辛普莱克斯码上实现确定性纠错与随机错误模型下的渐近可靠解码，而经典记忆接收机在相同条件下失败。

Abstract: Consider the task of identifying an unknown classical codeword delivered to a register of quantum sensor qubits through a sequence of weak phase interactions that each encode one bit. Suppose that each received bit controls an identical $Z$-rotation of angle $θ$ applied to every sensor qubit; receivers may also use auxiliary qubits within a total qubit budget $Q$, with arbitrary control unitaries and entanglement among all qubits allowed during each reception step. When the total collected signal is weak ($Qθ\rightarrow 0$), we establish that a sensor coupled to a persistent quantum memory can identify the original codeword more reliably than any receiver with the same $Q$ and $θ$ but only classical memory between received bits, even when bit flips occur during transmission. In particular, for a family of $[n,k]$ binary simplex codes with even $k$ and $n=2^k-1$, we achieve exact correction of up to $t\leq(k-2)/4$ transmission errors by coupling the sensor register to a persistent quantum memory of the same size, using $Q = Θ((t+1)\log n)$ and $θ=\frac{2π}{k\sqrt{n+1}}$; for $t=o(\sqrt k)$, every comparable receiver with only classical memory instead has average success probability (over uniformly chosen codewords) tending to zero. Furthermore, under independent bit flips of any fixed probability $p<1/16$, our receiver with persistent quantum memory achieves decoding error tending to zero as $n\to\infty$ using $Q=O(\log n\log\log n)$ total qubits and the same $θ$. In contrast, every otherwise equivalent receiver with only classical memory has vanishing average success probability under the same stochastic error model.

</details>


### [115] [Lie Algebraic Uncertainty Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03661)
*Xiaoli Hu,Naihuan Jing,Jian Zhang,Yunlong Xiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于协方差的量子不确定性表述，为厄米可观测量和非厄米算符给出无限族不确定性关系，并在李代数基下用代数与表示直接导出最优闭式下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子不确定性关系刻画可观测量的根本不相容性，但仅少数情形已知解析的最优态无关下界，且通常需要遍历量子态进行优化，缺乏统一、可计算的一般框架。

Method: 引入协方差形式的不确定性表述；针对构成李代数基的算符，利用代数结构及其表示直接进行代数计算，替代对量子态的优化；并在各权空间内分析不确定性取值。

Result: 得到同时适用于厄米可观测量与非厄米算符的无限族不确定性关系；在李代数基情形下获得最优界的闭式表达；在每个权空间内不确定性取固定值，使不等式转化为守恒律。

Conclusion: 交换关系及其在量子态上的实现共同决定不相容算符的最小量子不确定性；通过联系对称性与表示论，为从底层李代数揭示量子信息处理的固有限制提供了途径。

Abstract: Uncertainty relations express the fundamental incompatibility of quantum observables, yet optimal state-independent bounds are known analytically in only a few cases. Here we introduce a covariance-based formulation of quantum uncertainty that yields an infinite family of relations for both Hermitian observables and non-Hermitian operators. For operators forming a basis of a Lie algebra, we derive the optimal bound in closed form from the algebra and its representation, replacing optimization over quantum states with a direct algebraic calculation. Within each weight space, uncertainty takes a fixed value, turning the inequality into a conservation law. These results show how commutation relations and their realization on a quantum state jointly determine the minimum quantum uncertainty associated with incompatible operators. By connecting uncertainty to symmetry and representation theory, the framework offers a route to uncovering intrinsic limits on quantum information processing through its underlying Lie algebra.

</details>


### [116] [BARC codes: general polynomial framework for coherent-state superposition codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03663)
*Praneel Gore,Davit Aghamalyan,Varun Narasimhachar,Andrew Tanggara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出代数框架构造玻色代数受限星座（BARC）码，通过多元复多项式解集对称性约束相干态星座，并给出设计准则与性能基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 玻色量子纠错码需对光子增益/损耗误差具备近似Knill–Laflamme条件，现有星座类编码缺乏系统性代数构造与几何分离度分析框架。

Method: 以多元复多项式系统的解集对称性约束有限相干态叠加构造码字；将约束与光子增益和损耗误差关联以施加近似Knill–Laflamme条件；对等权星座点推导最小几何分离度上界；单模情形用正交群对称性刻画二次多项式解集，得到显式椭球与六角BARC码。

Result: 推导出等权星座点最小几何分离度上界；在单模下得到椭球与六角BARC码族的显式构造；在纯损耗噪声下用最优恢复的纠缠保真度，将代表性实例与球面码及cubature码进行基准比较。

Conclusion: BARC码提供了基于代数对称性的玻色纠错码新构造途径，其噪声鲁棒性可由最小几何分离度刻画，并在纯损耗场景下与既有星座码进行性能对标。

Abstract: We introduce an algebraic framework for constructing a new class of quantum error-correcting codes, bosonic algebraically-restricted constellation (BARC) codes. The code states are finite superpositions of coherent-state constellations constrained by symmetries of solution sets to multivariate complex polynomial systems. The constraints are associated with photon-gain and photon-loss errors, thereby imposing approximate Knill--Laflamme conditions for such errors.
