For equally-weighted constellation points, we derive upper bounds on the minimum geometric separation between points, indicating how noise-resilient the code is. In the single-mode case, we characterize degree-two polynomial solution sets using an orthogonal group symmetry to obtain explicit ellipsoidal and hexagonal BARC codes. We benchmark representative instances of these families against spherical and cubature codes using the entanglement fidelity with optimal recovery under pure-loss noise.

</details>


### [117] [Optimal kernel functions for linear combination of Hamiltonian simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03669)
*Harriet Apel,Bjorn K. Berntson*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文扩展了线性组合哈密顿量模拟(LCHS)的近似理论至更广的可允许核函数类,在标准块编码访问模型下证明了查询复杂度函数最小值的渐近展开,系数2e/π比Low-Somma的经验最优值改进了约2.1倍,并给出谱收敛求积构造保证离散化后仍保持改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LCHS为线性非幺正动力学提供渐近最优的量子模拟框架,但此前最优核函数的选择局限于特定族且依赖数值最小化,缺乏对更广核函数类的理论刻画与严格最优性证明。

Method: 将可允许核函数类推广至满足自然复分析条件的一类函数,建立LCHS近似定理;在时间无关块编码访问模型下构造使查询代价泛函最小的唯一核函数,基于Hardy空间理论与凸分析推导该泛函最小值的渐近展开;并构造谱收敛求积,给出算符误差与LCU次归一化的显式界。

Result: 证明了最优查询代价泛函的渐近展开为(2e/π)(log(1/ε) − log log(1/ε) + o(1)) (ε↓0),领先系数2e/π相比Low与Somma数值最小化所得系数改进约2.1倍;证明该渐近改进在离散化为有限酉算符线性组合后仍成立。

Conclusion: LCHS的核函数最优选择问题在更广函数类中获得严格解析解,确立了查询复杂度的可证明最优系数,并保证离散实现后的优势,为线性非幺正量子模拟提供理论最优框架。

Abstract: Linear combination of Hamiltonian simulation (LCHS) provides an asymptotically optimal framework for the quantum simulation of linear non-unitary dynamics. The LCHS approach approximately implements the Peano--Baker propagator through an integral representation determined by a kernel function. We establish an LCHS approximation theorem for a broadened class of admissible kernel functions, defined by natural complex-analytic conditions. In the standard time-independent block-encoding access model, we explicitly construct, for each target error $\varepsilon$, the unique kernel function in this class minimizing the query-cost functional and show that the minimum of this functional has the asymptotic expansion $\tfrac{2\mathrm{e}}π\big(\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}-\log\log\frac{1}{\varepsilon}+o(1)\big)$ as ${\varepsilon\downarrow0}$. The provably optimal leading coefficient $2\mathrm{e}/π$ improves by a factor of approximately 2.1 on the coefficient obtained empirically by Low and Somma through numerical minimization over their family of optimally scaling kernel functions. We construct a spectrally convergent quadrature with explicit bounds on operator error and linear combination of unitaries (LCU) subnormalization, proving that this asymptotic improvement persists after discretization into a finite linear combination of unitaries suitable for implementation. Our results are based on Hardy space theory and convex analysis.

</details>


### [118] [Classical Algorithms for Bipartite Quantum Max-Cut on Dense Expanders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03670)
*Stuart Wayland,Zackary Jorquera,Alexandra Kolla*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对稠密平衡二部扩展图上的反铁磁 Heisenberg 模型（等价于 Quantum Max-Cut），提出了多项式时间的经典随机算法，可加性误差逼近基态能量与任意基态边关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Quantum Max-Cut 是研究广泛的局部哈密顿量问题，其二部情形的经典复杂性仍为开放问题，本文旨在为该情形提供经典可解性证据。

Method: 在完全二部图的完美匹配集合上构造马尔可夫链，每个匹配对应单态的乘积态；该链收敛到基态，并利用图的扩展性证明多项式步数与采样即可收敛。算法为多项式时间经典随机算法。

Result: 算法在 n 与 1/ε 的多项式时间内，以加性误差 ε 估计基态能量及任意基态边关联；该结果适用于稠密平衡二部扩展图，包括每个固定 p>0 的 G(n,n,p)（高概率成立）。

Conclusion: 在稠密平衡二部扩展图上，Quantum Max-Cut（即 spin-1/2 反铁磁 Heisenberg 模型）具有多项式时间经典随机逼近算法。

Abstract: Quantum Max-Cut is a well-studied local Hamiltonian problem for which the classical complexity of the bipartite case remains open. We give a polynomial-time classical randomized algorithm for the spin-$\tfrac12$ antiferromagnetic Heisenberg model, equivalently Quantum Max-Cut, on dense balanced bipartite expanders. This class includes $G(n,n,p)$ with high probability for every fixed $p>0$. The algorithm estimates the ground energy and any ground state edge correlation to additive error $\varepsilon$ in time polynomial in $n$ and $1/\varepsilon$. We construct a Markov chain on perfect matchings of the complete bipartite graph, each matching representing a product of singlets, that converges to the ground state, and use the expansion of the graph to show that polynomially many steps and samples of this chain suffice.

</details>


### [119] [Quantum simulation of field-tunable spin spectroscopy of the quantum magnet Cs2CoCl4 on a trapped-ion quantum computer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03671)
*Elias Kokkas,Nora Bauer,Justin Provazza,Mark E. Nowakowski,Kathleen Hamilton,Gilles Buchs,Andrew Sornborger,Travis S. Humble,Ananth Kaushik,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用IonQ 36量子比特处理器，以仅420个双量子比特门的压缩线路模拟36格点XXZ链的磁激发谱与自旋动力学，重构结果与DMRG高度吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子磁体的集体激发谱是检验量子计算机研究强关联物质能力的直接途径，但硬件噪声限制了可用线路深度，因此需要探究线路压缩后能保留多少磁性信息。

Method: 在IonQ Forte Enterprise（36量子比特）上，针对横场下描述量子磁体Cs2CoCl4的36格点Heisenberg XXZ链，采用无辅助比特的局域kick协议结合经典张量网络压缩，将基态制备与时间演化编码进仅含420个双量子比特门的量子线路，并进行误差缓解。

Result: 重构的动力学结构因子呈现从宽双自旋子连续谱向色散分支（近预期临界场处具磁振子特征）的演化，并观察到最近邻自旋关联的场驱动符号反转；与DMRG相比，Pearson相关和结构相似度均高于0.9，归一化均方误差低于0.15，并与非弹性中子散射实验进行了对比。

Conclusion: 线路压缩与误差缓解可使含噪量子处理器同时恢复集体激发与局域磁关联，为量子谱学提供定量基准，并为推广至更复杂磁系统奠定基础。

Abstract: The collective excitation spectra of quantum magnets provide a direct test of quantum computers as tools for studying strongly correlated matter. Hardware noise limits the circuit depths available for these calculations, leaving the question of how much magnetic information can survive circuit compression. Using IonQ's 36 qubit Forte Enterprise processor, we simulate the magnetic excitation spectra and spin dynamics of a 36 site Heisenberg XXZ chain describing the quantum magnet Cs2CoCl4 in a transverse field using quantum circuits containing just 420 two qubit gates. The workflow combines an ancilla free local kick protocol with classical tensor network compression, encoding both ground state preparation and time evolution. The reconstructed dynamical structure factors capture the evolution from a broad two spinon continuum toward a dispersive branch with magnon character near the expected critical field. We observe an interesting field driven sign reversal of the nearest neighbor spin correlations which shows a complementary signature of this magnetic evolution. Our error mitigation methods bring the reconstructed total spectra into close agreement with density matrix renormalization group calculations, with Pearson correlation and structural similarity above 0.9 and normalized mean squared error below 0.15. We also perform comparisons with experimental inelastic neutron scattering measurements to connect the recovered excitations to the material's magnetic response. Our results demonstrate that circuit compression and error mitigation allow noisy quantum processors to recover both collective excitations and local magnetic correlations providing a quantitative benchmark for quantum spectroscopy and a basis for extending these methods to more complex magnetic systems.

</details>


### [120] [Ordering-Aware Theory of Trotter Error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03672)
*Zhenning Liu,Zhi-Yuan Wei,Michael Foss-Feig,Alexey V. Gorshkov,Andrew M. Childs*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文系统研究量子多体动力学模拟中乘积公式的哈密顿项排序效应，提出了可高效计算、对排序敏感的Trotter误差上界，并利用误差的局域结构将长链排序优化归约为加权de Bruijn图上的最小平均权重环问题。优化表明顺序（阶梯式）排序优于标准砖墙电路，深度受限时波状并行-顺序排序仍保持显著精度优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘积公式是模拟量子多体动力学的基本工具，但其应用中一个根本的自由度——哈密顿项排序——此前基本未被探索。作者希望量化排序对模拟精度的影响，并将排序作为一个可被利用的设计自由度以提高模拟精度。

Method: 1) 针对几何局域哈密顿量，构造一个可高效计算、对排序敏感、可支撑任意高阶乘积公式的有限步长Trotter误差上界。2) 在平移不变的一维链上验证该界与精确的二阶、四阶、六阶Trotter误差的接近程度。3) 利用误差界与估计的局域结构进行高效排序优化，将长链问题归约为加权de Bruijn图上的最小平均权重环问题。4) 在随机平移不变链上比较顺序（阶梯式）排序、标准砖墙电路以及波状并行-顺序排序。

Result: 在小系统测试中，所提界接近于精确的二阶、四阶和六阶Trotter误差；在大型系统上，相比此前的高阶解析界改进了数个数量级。对于随机平移不变链，顺序（阶梯式）排序一致优于标准砖墙电路；当电路深度受限时，优化器倾向于波状并行-顺序排序，且仍相对砖墙电路保留大部分精度优势。

Conclusion: 排序是理解和改进乘积公式模拟的一个有用的自由度，既能用于更准确地刻画Trotter误差，也能通过优化排序（乃至在深度约束下选择并行-顺序混合排序）显著提升模拟精度。

Abstract: Product formulas are a basic tool for simulating quantum many-body dynamics, but a fundamental degree of freedom in their application---the ordering of Hamiltonian terms---has remained largely unexplored. Here we systematically study such ordering effects, quantifying their impact on simulation accuracy and exploiting them to design more accurate product-formula simulations. We develop an efficiently computable, ordering-sensitive bound on the finite-step-size Trotter error for geometrically local Hamiltonians for arbitrarily high-order product formulas. For translation-invariant one-dimensional chains, our bounds are close to exact second-, fourth-, and sixth-order Trotter errors in small-system tests, while on large-scale systems they improve on previous higher-order analytical bounds by orders of magnitude. We further use the local structure of the error bounds and estimates to optimize ordering efficiently, reducing the long-chain problem to a minimum-mean-weight-cycle problem on a weighted de Bruijn graph. For random translation-invariant chains, sequential (staircase-like) orderings consistently outperform standard brickwall circuits; when circuit depth is restricted, the optimizer instead favors wave-like parallel-sequential orderings, which preserve much of the accuracy advantage over brickwall circuits. These results establish ordering as a useful degree of freedom for both understanding and improving product-formula simulation.

</details>


### [121] [Quantum Simulation on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03673)
*Dylan Herman,Jacob Watkins,Guneykan Ozgul,Jiayu Shen,Brandon Augustino,Junhyung Lyle Kim,Shouvanik Chakrabarti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究黎曼流形上薛定谔方程的量子模拟算法：一类基于Laplace–Beltrami算子本征基的全局谱方法，为环面、球面、单纯形、截断正象限及ℓ_p球等给出高效量子算法；另一类基于多图册局部谱方法的一致模拟，原则上适用于任意紧流形，并给出多项式谱截断的连续与离散分析，最后应用于优化与物理模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼流形上的量子模拟在优化与物理模拟中具有广泛需求，但已有方法多针对平直空间，缺乏对曲率与非欧几里得约束域的高效量子算法。论文旨在为黎曼薛定谔方程构造高效、具体的量子模拟算法，并拓展其在量子优化和场论模拟中的应用。

Method: 第一类算法为全局谱方法，通过识别到Laplace–Beltrami算子本征基的高效变换实现模拟，对环面、球面（标准度量）、单纯形（Wright–Fisher度量）、截断正象限及其可逆仿射像（对数障碍Hessian度量）、ℓ_p球（Duffy映射诱导度量）给出显式高效算法。第二类算法为基于多图册局部谱方法的一致模拟，适用于任意紧流形；先在连续情形分析并给出多项式谱截断充分条件，再对常维流形张量积情形做多项式谱截断的离散化分析。

Result: 给出环面、球面、单纯形、截断正象限与ℓ_p球等具体流形上的高效量子模拟算法；建立多图册局部谱方法的一致模拟框架及多项式谱截断的连续与离散条件；导出测地凸函数上量子哈密顿下降的推广与收敛分析（含球面与单纯形上的显式算法），给出实空间绝热量子算法的黎曼推广；证明算法可模拟某些空间离散场论，包括非线性sigma模型的一个变体。

Conclusion: 论文提出了覆盖特定流形与一般紧流形的两类黎曼薛定谔方程量子模拟算法，兼具显式高效性与理论适用性，并将方法成功应用于几何约束优化与场论物理模拟，为黎曼几何背景下的量子计算提供了统一框架与应用示范。

Abstract: We investigate algorithms for the quantum simulation of the Schrödinger equation on a Riemannian manifold, where the kinetic operator is defined by the Laplace--Beltrami operator corresponding to the metric. Our first algorithms are based on a global spectral method based on the identification of an efficient transform to the eigenbasis of the Laplace--Beltrami operator. We use this method to provide explicit, efficient, quantum simulation algorithms for the Riemannian Schrödinger equation on tori and spheres with their standard metrics, simplices with the Wright--Fisher metric, truncated positive orthants and their invertible affine images with the log-barrier Hessian metric, and $\ell_p$ balls with a metric induced by the Duffy map. Our second algorithm is based on a coherent simulation of local spectral methods on multiple charts, and is in principle applicable to any compact manifold. We first analyze this algorithm in the continuum and derive conditions under which a polynomial spectral cutoff suffices. We also provide a discretization analysis of a polynomial spectral cutoff for tensor-products of constant-dimensional manifolds. Finally, we consider applications of these methods to optimization and physical simulation. For optimization, we provide results including a generalization and convergence analysis of Quantum Hamiltonian Descent for geodesically convex functions that leads to explicit algorithms on the sphere and simplex, and a Riemannian generalization of the Real-Space Adiabatic Algorithm. For physical simulation, we show that our algorithms can simulate certain spatially discretized field theories, including a variant of the nonlinear sigma model.

</details>


### [122] [Efficient capacity-achieving entanglement generation with application to pure-loss Bosonic channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03674)
*Avijit Mandal,Christophe Piveteau,Henry D. Pfister,Joseph M. Renes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一种高效纠缠生成方案，可在广泛信道类上达到相干信息量，并对可退化信道（含截断纯损耗玻色信道）达到量子容量，是首个显式且可高效译码、达到能量约束下纯损耗玻色信道量子容量的协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子通信在噪声信道上的速率极限已被确立，但能高效达到这些极限的编码方案极少，这与经典信道中polar码和LDPC码均可高效达到经典容量形成鲜明对比。因此需要构造高效且可达极限的量子编码方案。

Method: 利用针对两类经典输入量子输出（CQ）信道的polar码：一类输出可交换，可用经典polar译码算法译码；另一类输出为纯态的heralded混合，采用带量子消息的置信传播（BPQM）算法译码。基于此构造高效纠缠生成方案。

Result: 所构造方案在广泛信道类上达到相干信息量；对可退化信道（包括截断版纯损耗玻色信道）达到量子容量。该构造被认为是首个显式且可高效译码、达到能量约束下纯损耗玻色信道量子容量的协议。

Conclusion: 该工作将polar码技术拓展到量子通信场景，为高效达到量子容量提供了显式可译码方案，并在纯损耗玻色信道等重要信道上实现了能量约束下的量子容量。

Abstract: While the ultimate limits on the rate of quantum communication over noisy channels are well-established, very few efficient coding schemes achieving these limits are known. This contrasts sharply with transmitting classical data over classical channels, where polar codes and low-density parity-check codes both efficiently achieve the classical capacity of any noisy channel. In this work we construct an efficient entanglement generation scheme which achieves the coherent information for a broad class of channels. For degradable channels in the class, which includes truncated versions of the pure loss Bosonic channel, the resulting scheme achieves the quantum capacity. The construction makes use of polar codes for two kinds of classical-input, quantum-output (CQ) channels: those with commuting outputs and those whose outputs are heralded mixtures of pure states. The former case is amenable to decoding by classical polar decoding algorithms, the latter by the belief propagation with quantum messages (BPQM) algorithm. To our knowledge, this construction is the first explicit and efficiently decodable protocol that achieves the energy-constrained quantum capacity of the pure-loss Bosonic channel.

</details>


### [123] [How I Learned to Stop Worrying and Love the Redfield Equation: Completely-Positive Resummation of Non-Markovian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03676)
*Greg Kaplanek,Jason Pollack*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将Redfield方程（含时系数TCL2方程）与同一二阶微扰映射的Magnus指数重求和进行对比，给出保证Redfield演化接近CPTP映射的弱耦合判据，并以量子比特系统为例加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Redfield方程是开放量子系统动力学最简单的微扰描述之一，但作为近似其地位微妙：微扰论逐阶固定约化动力学，而求解Redfield方程得到的重求和不保证完全正性。论文旨在澄清Redfield演化在何时、以及为何即使高度非Lindblad型仍可保持可靠。

Method: 比较Redfield演化与同一二阶微扰映射的指数化（Magnus）重求和，后者在一大类物理条件下满足CPTP；同时构造一个简单的CPTP预解式（resolvent）重求和，用以说明同一微扰映射重求和的非唯一性；并推导一个弱耦合判据。

Result: Redfield与Magnus方案在二阶一致，但在更高阶通过时间排序修正而不同；给出的弱耦合判据保证Redfield演化即使是非马尔可夫的也保持与某个CPTP映射接近；在有限维（量子比特）系统的两个例子中验证了该判据。

Conclusion: 结果阐明了Redfield演化在高度非Lindbladian情形下仍可保持可靠的条件与原因，并揭示了同一微扰映射重求和的非唯一性。

Abstract: The Redfield equation with time-dependent coefficients (or TCL2 equation) gives one of the simplest perturbative descriptions of open quantum system dynamics derived from a microscopic theory. Its status as an approximation is nevertheless subtle: while perturbation theory fixes the reduced dynamics order-by-order in the system-environment coupling, solving the Redfield equation yields a particular resummation that need not preserve complete positivity. Here we compare Redfield evolution with an exponentiated (Magnus) resummation of the same second-order perturbative map, which is completely positive and trace preserving (CPTP) for a broad class of physical conditions. We also construct a simple CPTP resolvent resummation, illustrating the non-uniqueness of resummations of the same perturbative map. The Redfield and Magnus prescriptions agree to second order but differ through time-ordering corrections at higher orders. We exhibit a weak-coupling criterion which guarantees that Redfield evolution, even when non-Markovian, remains close to a CPTP map. We illustrate the criterion in two examples, focusing on finite-dimensional (qubit) systems. Our results clarify when, and why, Redfield evolution can remain reliable even when it is highly non-Lindbladian.

</details>


### [124] [Exact Recovery for Non-Abelian Surface Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03677)
*Alison Warman,Nathanan Tantivasadakarn,Sakura Schafer-Nameki*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究基于任意有限群 $G$ 的量子双重 $D(G)$ 的非阿贝尔拓扑表面码的精确纠错，构造了正交误差基与确定性恢复协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔拓扑表面码的纠错比阿贝尔情形更复杂：误差基、稳定子冗余以及恢复操作的构造尚不完善，本文旨在给出适用于任意有限群的一般性精确恢复方案。

Method: 确定由群乘法算符和不可约表示算符构成的正交误差基；当误差支撑在格或对偶格的闭合回路上时，因稳定子存在导致基冗余，为此引入规范固定（gauge-fixing）在码空间上得到完备正交误差基；在假定给定中性可纠误差团簇输入下，构造电荷转移电路与通量转移电路，将误差转移到辅助比特上再投影消除。

Result: 获得了码空间上的完备正交误差基，并构建了电荷与通量转移电路，实现将误差移至辅助比特并投影消除的精确确定性恢复协议，适用于任意有限群（尤其非阿贝尔群）的表面码。

Conclusion: 该工作为任意有限群 $D(G)$ 的非阿贝尔拓扑表面码提供了统一的精确纠错框架，通过规范固定与电荷/通量转移电路实现确定性恢复，推广了表面码纠错理论。

Abstract: We study exact recovery for non-Abelian topological surface codes based on the quantum double $D(G)$ of any finite group $G$. We determine an orthogonal error basis, that is comprised of group multiplication and irreducible representation operators. Whenever the errors are supported on closed loops on the lattice or dual lattice, there is a redundancy in the basis, due to stabilizers. To remedy this, we introduce a gauge-fixing that results in a complete and orthogonal error basis on the code space. Assuming as input predetermined, neutral correctable error clusters, we construct charge and flux transfer circuits that move the errors onto ancillas that then get projected out. This is an exact and deterministic recovery protocol, applicable to surface codes for any finite, in particular non-Abelian, group $G$.

</details>


### [125] [Low-Overhead Quantum Error Correction with Boundary-Connected Planar Modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03682)
*Oscar Higgott,Hasan Sayginel,Francisco J. H. Heras,Zhiyang He,Tomas Jochym-O'Connor,Andrew W. Senior,Lei M. Zhang,Thomas Edlich,James S. Spencer,Matt McEwen,Craig Gidney,Johannes Bausch,Pushmeet Kohli,Hartmut Neven*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作通过将量子处理器划分为平面模块并用稀疏静态边界连接，构造模块化双曲/色码，显著降低量子纠错的物理比特开销并实现高编码率与高距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实用量子计算机需要量子纠错，但最广泛实现的平面表面码存在巨大的物理量子比特开销。作者希望在不牺牲平面模块制造优势的前提下，降低开销并提升编码效率。

Method: 将量子处理器划分为可管理的平面模块，通过稀疏、静态的模块间边界连接，基于新的半双曲码族构造模块化双曲表面码和色码；采用模块化辛ndrome提取电路，并结合神经网络解码器与匹配解码器进行电路级模拟；进一步设计用于逻辑操作的容错walking电路，与低权重、高对称逻辑基及模块化提取系统协同设计。

Result: 电路级模拟显示，即使在较高的模块间接缝错误率下，该模块化存储设计可将物理量子比特开销较表面码降低十倍以上。外推至更大系统，编码率 k/n=1/16、距离 d≥22 的模块码在编码率和距离上均超过 [[288,12,18]] 双gross双变量自行车码，且主要使用平面模块内的最近邻门，暗示相对表面码超过30倍的开销降低。模块化提取系统比码本身小约4.5倍。

Conclusion: 该模块化存储可作为通用容错架构中的密集存储，在不牺牲平面模块制造优势的情况下释放高码率码的效率，为实现实用量子计算机提供可行路径。

Abstract: Realizing practical quantum computers requires quantum error correction, but the most widely implemented approach, the planar surface code, demands a substantial physical qubit overhead. Here, we demonstrate that partitioning quantum processors into manageable, flat modules with non-local inter-module connections provides a natural solution. By wiring sparse, static boundary connections between Euclidean planar modules, we construct modular hyperbolic surface and color codes, based on new families of semi-hyperbolic codes. Using modular syndrome extraction circuits and efficient neural network and matching decoders, our circuit-level simulations show that this modular memory design can reduce the physical qubit overhead by tenfold or more compared to the surface code, even under elevated inter-module seam error rates. Projecting to larger system sizes, we find modular codes with encoding rate $k/n=1/16$ and distance $d\geq 22$, exceeding both the rate and distance of the $[[288,12,18]]$ two-gross bivariate bicycle code while using mostly nearest-neighbor gates within planar modules, and implying an over $30\times$ overhead reduction relative to surface codes. We develop fault-tolerant walking circuits for implementing logic via code automorphisms, which we co-design with low-weight, highly symmetrical logical bases and a modular extractor system that is $\approx 4.5\times$ smaller than the code itself. Combining these methods, our modular memory can be used as dense storage in a universal fault-tolerant architecture, unlocking the efficiency of high-rate codes without sacrificing the fabrication advantages of planar modules.

</details>


### [126] [Modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality for 1D non-commuting Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03683)
*Ángela Capel,David Pérez-García,Matteo Scandi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文首次在非对易情形下证明了热浴动力学的修正对数Sobolev不等式（MLSI），常数约为Ω(1/log n)，并由此推出至Gibbs态的快速混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在非对易量子多体系统中，热浴/Gibbs采样器的混合时间缺乏严格保证；已有MLSI结果主要限于对易或特定可解模型，非对易情形尚无证明。

Method: 针对1D有限自旋链上的有限程非对易Hamiltonian，结合相对熵的弱准因子分解、关联衰减估计、一致的逐条件局部gap，以及条件熵与二次能量之间的带缓冲（buffered）比较；最后利用全局gap消除有界的熵缺陷，从而建立热浴动力学、准局域Gibbs采样器及正则化热浴动力学的MLSI。

Result: 证明了常数约为Ω(1/log n)的MLSI，两个采样器在相对熵和迹范数下快速收敛到Gibbs态，混合时间为O(log n·log(n/ε))；且允许相互作用在空间上非均匀。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在非对易区域给出MLSI及相应的多项式（对数）混合时间刻画，为非对易量子Gibbs采样提供了理论工具。

Abstract: Given a finite-range, non-commuting Hamiltonian on a 1D chain of finite spins, we prove a modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality with constant $Ω(1/\log n)$ for the heat-bath dynamics, a quasi-local Gibbs sampler, and for the regularised heat-bath dynamics. This implies rapid mixing of both samplers towards the Gibbs state in relative entropy and trace norm, with mixing times of order $\mathcal{O}(\log n\cdot \log(n/ε))$. The interactions may be spatially inhomogeneous. The proof combines weak quasi-factorisation of relative entropy, decay-of-correlation estimates, a uniform conditional local gap, and a buffered comparison between conditional entropy and quadratic energy. The resulting bounded entropy defect is removed using the global gap. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first proof of MLSI in the non-commuting regime.

</details>


### [127] [Single-Shot Error Correction at Optimal Spacetime Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03684)
*Kishor Bharti,Tobias Haug,Derek Khu,Andrew Tanggara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：在支持长程连通性与快速可靠经典处理的硬件上，使用量子 Tanner 码与显式含噪纠错电路及高效解码，可实现 Ω(S(K+log(S/ε))) 的匹配时空开销，可靠性仅带来所有量子比特共享的对数级额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作给出独立擦除信道下存储 K 个逻辑量子比特 S 步、误差不超过 ε 所需时空开销的下界 Ω(S(K+log(S/ε)))，并给出匹配构造，但该构造假设纠错是理想无误的。本文旨在去掉这一理想假设，证明在显式含噪纠错电路与高效解码下同样能达到该最优标度。

Method: 采用量子 Tanner 码构造存储方案；每个存储步骤包含一轮错误校验子提取（syndrome extraction），随后进行固定次数的并行经典解码步骤，将残余误差降到足以保证后续故障仍可纠正的水平，且该次数与存储规模、存储时间及目标精度无关。分析覆盖足够弱但一般的电路噪声，包括校验子提取过程中的故障与相关故障；要求硬件具备长程量子比特连通性与快速可靠的经典处理。

Result: 在所有状态制备、门、测量与等待位置均计入成本的前提下，达到了与下界匹配的 Ω(S(K+log(S/ε))) 标度；每步失效概率随步数指数小，而每步存储开销与码长成线性关系；可靠性带来的额外开销仅为对数级且由所有存储量子比特共享。对独立擦除信道，该构造与已知最优下界在常数因子内一致。分析还扩展至某些常数深度逻辑 Clifford 操作。

Conclusion: 在含噪纠错电路与高效解码下，量子 Tanner 码可在保留最优时空标度的同时实现可靠量子存储，可靠性代价仅为共享的对数级开销，并将结果推广到部分常数深度逻辑 Clifford 操作。

Abstract: Recent work established that, for independent erasures, storing $K$ logical qubits for $S$ time steps with error at most $\varepsilon$ requires a spacetime cost of $Ω(S(K+\log(S/\varepsilon)))$. A matching construction was also given, but assumes ideal error correction. Here we show that the same scaling can be achieved with an explicit noisy error-correction circuit and efficient decoding, counting all state-preparation, gate, measurement, and wait locations, provided that the hardware supports long-range qubit connectivity and fast, reliable classical processing. Thus, reliability adds only a logarithmic overhead shared by all stored qubits. The construction works for sufficiently weak but otherwise general circuit noise, including faults during syndrome extraction and correlated faults, and for independent erasures it matches the best known lower bound up to constant factors. We attain this scaling using quantum Tanner codes, with each memory step consisting of one round of syndrome extraction followed by a fixed number of parallel classical decoder steps that reduce the residual error enough to keep later faults correctable, regardless of memory size, storage time, or target accuracy. Together, this gives exponentially small failure probability per step while keeping the cost of each memory step linear in the code size. The same analysis also extends to certain constant-depth logical Clifford operations.

</details>


### [128] [Efficient Block Encoding of Structured Hamiltonians by Separating Where and What](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03686)
*Alessandro Summer,François Jamet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于permute-act-unpermute结构的容错块编码电路，利用算子支撑几何降低SELECT的T门开销，并给出两体支撑下CSWAP网络的最优性证明及bridged CSWAP优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错块编码中非Clifford（T门）成本是资源瓶颈。既有方法在SELECT环节的T门数随哈密顿量项数增长，需要利用支撑几何实现随系统规模而非项数的T门开销，且不依赖平移对称或系数可因式分解。

Method: 构造permute-act-unpermute电路：用CSWAP网络将选定的支撑态搬运到固定目标寄存器，施加共享的局部电路，再撤销置换。针对两体支撑，在site transposition地址控制网络中证明并达到最小CSWAP数。引入bridged CSWAP，在目标作用期间保留并修复临时AND，修复为Clifford时可减半匹配正-逆CSWAP对的T门数，代价为多保留一个工作量子比特。

Result: 对Pauli项编译后使用8SN+O(log N)个T门和O(log N)个置换工作量子比特，N为系统量子比特数，S由支撑几何决定（最近邻S=1，全对全S=3/2）。海森堡环的完整块编码查询T门数随系统增大降低近3倍；五轨道Anderson杂质模型（混合最近邻与全对全几何）在大浴尺寸下降低约1.7倍，均在块编码归一化不变、峰值ancilla数相当的条件下与最低成本编译基线相比。

Conclusion: 通过利用支撑几何的permute-act-unpermute电路与bridged CSWAP，可将SELECT的T门开销与支撑几何挂钩而非项数，在容错块编码中实现显著T门降低，同时保持归一化与ancilla成本可比。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant block encodings of structured Hamiltonians can reduce their non-Clifford cost in SELECT by selecting where an operator acts separately from what is applied. We construct permute-act-unpermute circuits whose CSWAP networks exploit the support geometry: a selected support is brought to a fixed target register, a shared local circuit acts there, and the permutation is undone. For supports of fixed size, the $T$ count of SELECT then grows with the system size rather than with the number of terms, without requiring translational symmetry or factorised coefficients. For two-site supports, we establish and attain the minimum CSWAP count within address-controlled networks of site transpositions. Compiled for Pauli terms, these circuits use $8SN+O(\log N)$ $T$ gates with $O(\log N)$ permutation work qubits, where $N$ is the number of system qubits and $S$ is determined by the support geometry, with $S=1$ for nearest-neighbour interactions and $S=3/2$ for all-to-all pairs. We also introduce a bridged CSWAP that retains and repairs a temporary AND across the target action. When the repair is Clifford, it halves the $T$ count of a matched forward-inverse CSWAP pair at the cost of one retained work qubit. For a Heisenberg ring, the complete block-encoding query reduces the $T$ count by a factor approaching 3 as the system size grows. For a five-orbital Anderson impurity model, which combines nearest-neighbour and all-to-all support geometries, the reduction is about 1.7 at large bath sizes. Both comparisons use the lowest-cost compiled baselines considered, at unchanged block-encoding normalisation and comparable peak ancilla counts.

</details>


### [129] [Universal Bounds for Out-of-Distribution Unitary Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03690)
*Joachim Favre,Armando Angrisani,Zoë Holmes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于输入分布一阶与二阶矩的量子动力学分布外学习框架，用"偏置"与"表达性"刻画训练与测试分布间的泛化能力，并给出一系列可学性与不可学性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：未知幺正动力学在一族态上的作用，能在多大程度上揭示它在其他态上的作用，即为量子动力学的分布外泛化建立定量理论。

Method: 以训练与测试纯态分布的一阶矩和二阶矩为工具，定义系综的偏置（bias）与表达性（expressivity）两个量，据此分析任意训练—测试分布对之间的泛化，并考察张量积结构与单量子比特风险。

Result: 在随机实态上学习幺正作用即可推广到随机复态；无偏纯态系综的张量积保持表达性；单量子比特风险与偏置无关；早期局部加扰结果被作为松散特例重新导出，并给出紧的乘积分布到Haar分布界。

Conclusion: 论文以若干no-go结果和受限的泛化界收尾，说明幺正性在实现分布外泛化中具有不可替代的作用，而对一般信道则泛化能力有限。

Abstract: How much can the action of an unknown unitary dynamics on one family of states reveal about its action elsewhere? We develop a general framework for out-of-distribution quantum dynamics learning based on the first and second moments of the input distribution. These define a notion of the bias and expressivity of an ensemble, which together control generalization between arbitrary pure-state training and testing distributions. Our framework shows that only studying the action of a unitary on random real states can learn its action on random complex states, that tensor products of unbiased pure-state ensembles preserve expressivity, and that single-qubit risks are independent of bias. It also recovers earlier locally scrambling results as loose special cases and yields tight product-to-Haar bounds. We conclude with a series of no-go results and limited generalization bounds for learning certain channels, which further serve to underscore the distinctive role of unitarity in enabling out-of-distribution generalization.

</details>


### [130] [An operational characterization of finite-dimensional quantum theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03694)
*Lionel J. Dmello,Xiangling Xu,Marc-Olivier Renou,David Gross*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造有限个两体关联，证明唯一能同时满足"实现这些关联"且"在迭代隐形传态下稳定"的概率论是量子理论，从而在有限维情形下实现量子理论的自检验，并可认证超越已观测的Bell不等式违背。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学基础的一个核心目标是找出刻画物理理论的运算性约束。Bell不等式刻画了经典概率论，Tsirelson界是量子力学的第一步。本文关注其对偶问题：能否证明量子理论所预言的所有关联都确实可实现（即可被认证）。

Method: 构造有限个两体关联，要求满足两个条件：(1) 实现这些关联；(2) 在迭代隐形传态（iterated teleportation）下保持稳定。对于 (C^d)^{⊗n}，条件(1)可用 poly(d,n) 个测量设置验证；条件(2)可理解为对每个隐形传态步数 N 的层级测试。

Result: 证明唯一同时满足条件(1)和(2)的概率论就是量子理论。条件(1)的验证复杂度为 poly(d,n)。条件(2)构成一个层级测试体系。由此可认证存在比任何已直接观测到的更大的Bell不等式违背。

Conclusion: 在某种意义上，有限维量子理论可以被自检验（self-tested）：通过有限个两体关联及其在迭代隐形传态下的稳定性，可将量子理论唯一地辨识出来。

Abstract: A key goal in the foundations of quantum mechanics is to identify operational constraints characterizing physical theories. Bell inequalities do so for classical probability theory, while Tsirelson's bound provides a first step for quantum mechanics. Here, we address the dual question: Can one certify that all correlations predicted by quantum theory are actually realizable? In this work, we construct a finite number of two-body correlations such that the only probabilistic theory that (1) realizes them, and (2) does so in a way that is stable under iterated teleportation, is quantum theory. For $(\mathbb{C}^d)^{\otimes n}$, Condition (1) can be verified using $\operatorname{poly}(d,n)$ measurement settings. Condition (2) may be understood as a hierarchy of tests, one for each number $N$ of teleportation steps. There is thus a sense in which finite-dimensional quantum theory can be self-tested. In particular, one can certify the existence of Bell inequality violations larger than any that have been directly observed.

</details>


### [131] [Maximum-Entropy Extension of Quantum Correlation Functions from Short Real-Time Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03696)
*Filippo Maria Gambetta,Sabrina Yue Wang,Raul A. Santos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于平稳随机过程协方差序列理论的量子关联函数时间序列延拓与谱密度重构方法，利用Burg最大熵原理得到与已测值最一致的全极点自回归延拓，保证半正定性与谱正性，适用于标量与矩阵关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子关联函数谱可揭示准粒子与集体激发，但达到足够谱分辨率所需的长时间演化对经典与量子模拟都难以实现，因此需要从短时数据可靠地延拓时间序列并重构谱密度。

Method: 将量子关联函数与平稳随机过程的协方差序列建立联系，据此应用Burg最大熵原理求取与已测关联函数值兼容的"最少承诺"延拓；该延拓对应全极点自回归（AR）模型，其系数通过求解Yule--Walker方程得到；方法推广至矩阵值关联函数。

Result: 所得延拓按构造为半正定，从而保证谱的正性并自动满足总谱权重求和规则；可处理标量与矩阵关联函数，包括高度结构化或连续谱；矩阵推广可用额外测量的关联函数换取模拟时间，从而在短时、含噪数据下实现准确的延拓与谱重构。

Conclusion: 基于最大熵/Burg原理的自回归延拓为量子关联函数谱重构提供了有原则的框架，兼顾物理约束（正性、求和规则）与实用性，特别适合短时、含噪测量条件下的标量及矩阵关联函数分析。

Abstract: Correlation functions are central to understanding the behavior of quantum systems, with their spectra giving direct access to quasiparticle and collective excitations. However, the long-time evolution required for sufficient spectral resolution is challenging to reach for both classical and quantum simulations. In this work, we introduce a principled approach for extending time series of quantum correlation functions and reconstructing their spectral densities, based on their connection with covariance sequences of stationary stochastic processes. This connection enables us to apply Burg's maximum-entropy principle to find the least-committal extension compatible with the measured values of the correlation function, which corresponds to an all-pole autoregressive model whose coefficients solve a set of Yule--Walker equations. The resulting extension is positive semi-definite by construction, guaranteeing the positivity of the spectrum and the automatic satisfaction of its total spectral weight sum rule. The approach applies to both scalar and matrix-valued correlation functions, including those with highly structured or continuous spectra. The generalization to the matrix case makes it possible to trade simulation time for additional measured correlation functions, enabling accurate time-series extension and spectral reconstruction from short-time data, even in the presence of noise.

</details>


### [132] [On The Complexity of Redundancy-Free Quantum Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03697)
*Matthew B. Hastings,Alexander Schmidhuber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究无冗余哈密顿量的热态与基态复杂度，揭示其难度在 β²d 尺度上呈现经典易解／NP难／QMA完全的相变结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类哈密顿量自然出现在 Hamiltonian Decoded Quantum Interferometry (Hamiltonian DQI) 中，该框架把量子态制备的困难性拆为经典解码与无冗余哈密顿量热场双态制备两步；因此刻画其复杂度既能满足内在理论兴趣，也有助于理解 Hamiltonian DQI 状态制备步的复杂度。

Method: 以反交换图度 d 与逆温 β 的乘积 β²d 为复杂度控制参数；对小 β²d 构造近似配分函数的经典高效算法；对大 β²d 归约证明配分函数近似为 NP 难；引入"反交换玻璃"（frutration 纯由反交换关系产生）；给出小 β²d 下制备热场态的亚指数时间量子算法，并提出基于 Feiguin-Klich 哈密顿量的多项式时间算法路线。

Result: 相比一般哈密顿量，问题在平方级更低的温度下即变易解，复杂度由 β²d 决定；小 β²d 时经典高效近似配分函数，大 β²d 时近似配分函数 NP 难；Feiguin-Klich 哈密顿量给出在度 d 相互作用图上、β≲1/d 时制备一般哈密顿量热场双态的多项式时间算法；估计无冗余哈密顿量基态能量为 QMA 完全。

Conclusion: 无冗余哈密顿量的难度存在以 β²d 为标度的清晰相变：低温经典难解（NP 难），高温经典易解，基态能量估计达到 QMA 完全；这为 Hamiltonian DQI 的状态制备复杂度提供了精细刻画，并给出了潜在的多项式时间热场态制备途径。

Abstract: We consider a class of redudancy-free Hamiltonians, which are those where the trace vanishes for any product of Hamiltonian terms in which at least one term appears an odd number of times. This includes Hamiltonians which are sums of products of Paulis with no relations between them. These Hamiltonians arise naturally in Hamiltonian Decoded Quantum Interferometry, which separates the hardness of preparing quantum states into two steps: a classical decoding step, and a step preparing the thermofield double state of such a redundancy-free Hamiltonian.
  We focus on the hardness of these Hamiltonians, both for intrinsic interest and to understand the complexity of the state preparation step in Hamiltonian DQI. We present several results showing that the problem becomes easy at quadratically lower temperature than for a general Hamiltonian, with the complexity at given inverse temperature $β$ depending on $β^2 d$, where $d$ is the degree of the anticommutation graph. We give an efficient classical algorithm for small $β^2 d$ to approximate the partition function, while for large $β^2 d$ we show that approximating the partition function is NP-hard. To this end, we introduce what we call an anticommutation glass, where frustration arises purely from anticommutation relations. We give a subexponential time quantum algorithm to prepare the thermofield state for small $β^2 d$, and present some results toward a polynomial time algorithm based on the Feiguin-Klich Hamiltonian. As a result of possible independent interest, we show that this Hamiltonian leads to a polynomial time algorithm for preparing thermofield double states of general Hamiltonians on an interaction graph of degree $d$ for $β\lesssim 1/d$. Finally, we show that estimating the ground state energy of redundancy-free Hamiltonians is QMA-complete.

</details>


### [133] [Exponential lower bounds on the fermionic Gaussian rank of magic states and the bosonic coherent state rank of Fock states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03699)
*Oliver Reardon-Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了费米子高斯分解 |M⟩^{⊗k}（|M⟩ 为 4 量子比特 magic state）需要 Ω(1.4^k) 项，给出了首个超越常数的费米子-高斯秩下界，并得到近似秩下界为精确秩下界乘以 (1−δ²)；同时确定了 n 模玻色 Fock 态的相干态边界秩恰为 ∏_j(1+m_j)，回答了文献[1]的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典模拟量子力学算法的超多项式复杂度由 magic state 张量积分解为"自由态"叠加所需项数（秩）决定，但关于稳定子秩、费米子-高斯秩等分解项数的认知极少。除复杂度理论预期其随张量因子数指数增长外，稳定子秩最好下界仅为二次，费米子-高斯秩除固定常数外无任何下界。本文旨在填补这一空白并提供更强的下界工具。

Method: 作者针对费米子线性光学的标准 magic state |M⟩（可实现 swap 门的 4 量子比特态），分析其张量积 |M⟩^{⊗k} 的费米子高斯分解项数，证明精确秩下界为 Ω(1.4^k)，并推广至任意 k 个固定宇称非高斯态之积。对近似秩，证明 δ-近似秩下界为精确秩下界乘以 (1−δ²)。此外，利用同一方法分析 n 模玻色 Fock 态（模 j 含 m_j 个玻色子）的相干态边界秩，给出精确值 ∏_j(1+m_j)，并由此导出近似相干态秩的下界（为同一量乘以保真度函数）。

Result: 1) 任意费米子高斯分解 |M⟩^{⊗k} 至少含 Ω(1.4^k) 项，这是费米子-高斯秩的首个非常数下界；2) δ-近似秩下界为精确秩下界乘以 (1−δ²)；3) 精确秩结果直接适用于任意 k 个固定宇称非高斯态之积，近似秩结论则不适用；4) n 模玻色 Fock 态的相干态边界秩恰为 ∏_j(1+m_j)，证实文献[1]的猜想，并给出近似相干态秩下界为同一量乘以保真度函数。

Conclusion: 本文建立了费米子-高斯秩的指数级下界（Ω(1.4^k)）及其近似版本，并精确确定了玻色 Fock 态的相干态边界秩，验证了已有猜想。所提方法具有广泛适用性，可推广至多种 magic state 分解秩的下界分析，为经典模拟量子算法的复杂度理解提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Recent algorithms for classical simulation of quantum mechanics have runtime whose superpolynomial component is given by a linear dependence on the number of terms required to write large tensor products of certain "magic" states as superpositions of "free" states (which may be stabilizer states, fermionic Gaussian states or others). Surprisingly little is known about the number of terms in such decompositions, called ranks (e.g. the stabilizer rank, fermionic-Gaussian rank etc.). For complexity theoretic reasons they are expected to grow exponentially in the number of tensor factors but, while exponential upper bounds are known for both the stabilizer and fermionic-Gaussian rank of magic states; the best known lower bounds on the stabiliser rank are quadratic, and no bounds on the fermionic-Gaussian rank are known beyond fixed constants. In this work we prove that any fermionic Gaussian decomposition of $\lvert M\rangle^{\otimes k}$ consists of $Ω(1.4^k)$ terms. Here $\lvert M\rangle$ is the most standard magic state for fermionic linear optics: the $4$ qubit state that may be consumed to implement a swap gate. We also prove essentially matching bounds on the $δ$-approximate rank of the same state, lower bounding it by the same quantity that bounds the exact rank, multiplied by a factor of $1-δ^2$. Our results on exact fermionic Gaussian rank apply directly to any product of $k$ fixed parity non-Gaussian states, although the same is not true of the approximate rank. Finally, we prove that the coherent state border rank of an $n$-mode bosonic Fock state with $m_j$ bosons in mode $j$ is exactly $\prod_{j}(1+m_j)$, answering a conjecture of Ref. [1] and obtain lower bounds on the approximate coherent state rank given by the same quantity multiplied by a function of the fidelity of the approximation, emphasizing the broad applicability of the method we employ.

</details>


### [134] [Scalable Passive QRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03700)
*Siddhartha Jain,Alexander M. Dalzell,Connor T. Hann*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可扩展的被动式物理QRAM实现蓝图，在原理上证明了其可行性。该方案每次查询能耗为O(log N)，运行时间为O(log^2 N)，是首个达到polylog N能耗的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子随机存取存储器（QRAM）在量子算法中具有重要作用，但现有实现方案普遍面临能耗过高、难以规模化的问题。作者希望实现一种可扩展、被动（低能耗）的QRAM物理操作方案。

Method: 通过演化一个预先制备好的、时间无关的4-局域哈密顿量来实现QRAM查询。该哈密顿量包含O(N)个项，且每个量子比特最多参与4个项，其中N=2^n为n量子比特地址对应的存储位数。

Result: 该构造实现了每次QRAM查询O(log N)的能耗和O(log^2 N)的运行时间，是首个使用polylog N能耗的构造，同时保持被动性与可扩展性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种原理上可行的、可扩展且被动的物理QRAM实现蓝图，在能耗方面达到polylog N，为QRAM的实际物理实现提供了新思路。

Abstract: We present a blueprint for implementing the physical QRAM operation in a scalable, passive manner, proving its feasibility in principle. Our construction is passive because for an $n$-qubit address over $N = 2^n$ memory bits the energy cost per QRAM query is $O(\log N)$, and to our knowledge it is the first construction to use $\operatorname{polylog} N$ energy. The runtime for each query is $O(\log^2 N)$. It is achieved by evolving a pre-manufactured time-independent 4-local Hamiltonian with $O(N)$ terms where every qubit participates in at most 4 terms.

</details>


### [135] [Unitary complexity in polynomial space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03705)
*William Kretschmer,Ewin Tang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：若量子承诺存在，则要么酉合成问题无多项式时间解，要么BPP≠NEXP，表明无条件证明量子承诺存在需解决至少一个长期未决的复杂性理论问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子承诺存在性与酉合成问题、经典复杂性类分离之间的深层联系，揭示无条件证明量子承诺存在的困难性，并厘清酉复杂性类的合理定义。

Method: 提出unitP与unitPSPACE的新定义（分别处理可擦除与不可擦除垃圾），证明一般性引理：unitPSPACE中的每个酉要么相对任何经典预言机都不能高效合成，要么可用NEXP搜索预言机高效合成；并给出unitPSPACE的等价刻画——其矩阵元可在多项式空间内以任意精度计算。

Result: 由一般性引理推出主定理；unitPSPACE具有矩阵元多项式空间可计算的等价刻画，可通用擦除垃圾；但该性质相对酉预言机不成立；unitPSPACE相关的预言机分离将蕴含NC≠NP等经典下界。

Conclusion: 新定义在垃圾擦除、等价刻画等方面具有鲁棒性且使证明更简洁；无条件证明量子承诺存在需至少解决酉合成多项式时间可解性或BPP≠NEXP之一。

Abstract: We show that if quantum commitments exist, then either there is no polynomial-time solution to the unitary synthesis problem, or $\mathsf{BPP} \neq \mathsf{NEXP}$. Thus, showing unconditionally that quantum commitments exist would require answering at least one of two longstanding open questions in complexity theory. We prove our main result as a consequence of a more general lemma, which shows that every unitary in $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ either cannot be synthesized efficiently relative to any classical oracle, or can be synthesized efficiently with an oracle for $\mathsf{NEXP}$ search problems. Our lemma has other noteworthy consequences, including that certain oracle separations involving $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ would imply breakthrough classical lower bounds such as $\mathsf{NC} \neq \mathsf{NP}$.
  Along the way, we propose new definitions for the unitary complexity classes $\mathsf{unitaryP}$ and $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$. Our changes address the biggest conceptual issues with definitions suggested in prior work, and lead to elegant proofs. We study both implementations that erase garbage and implementations that allow it, because we cannot rule out the possibility that the two definitions differ. Nevertheless, we show that both definitions can be viewed as special cases of each other. We also showcase many other ways in which our definitions are robust. For example, we show that $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ has an equivalent characterization as the set of unitary transformations whose entries can be computed to arbitrary precision in polynomial space. Consequently, we deduce that $\mathsf{unitaryPSPACE}$ can generically erase garbage, a result that provably fails relative to unitary oracles.

</details>


### [136] [Quantum estimation, channel orders, and private capacity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03706)
*Christoph Hirche*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用measured-$\chi^2$散度与binary estimation把信道序（less-noisy、relative-entropy、quantum $\chi^2$）与"零私密容量信道可激活"的构造联系起来，给出有限码可靠性–保密性界，并对Werner–Holevo信道等确定私密容量与antidegradability的精确阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有例子表明零私密容量既不意味着antidegradable，两个零私密容量信道联合也可传输私密信息（激活现象）。现有entropic channel orders通过比较接收者与其环境能学到的信息来界定容量，但尚不清楚这些序与近期构造之间的关系，也不清楚为何"完全比较"可阻止与antidegradable辅助者的激活，而仅用regularized比较则不能。

Method: 以binary estimation为桥梁，其最小均方误差由measured $\chi^2$散度控制；给出积分表示，使qubit扩展上的measured $\chi^2$可恢复quantum $\chi^2$，从而由complete measured-$\chi^2$序推出complete quantum-$\chi^2$、relative-entropy与less-noisy序。用Hermitian-smoothed measured-$\chi^2$序刻画Zhu–Wang型signed lift（含量子参考）的存在性。以每blocklength的环境优势导出有限码可靠性–保密性界。结合qutrit例子，对Werner–Holevo信道族确定阈值并在非degradable区计算精确complementary capacities，将Pauli half-erasure激活推广至$1/2\le p<1$，并构造新的四能级族。用reference-assisted measured-$\chi^2$ witness说明这些激活构造缺乏complete comparison。

Result: 建立一个统一框架：complete comparison可阻止与antidegradable辅助者的激活，而仅regularized比较不能；Hermitian-smoothed measured-$\chi^2$序刻画signed lift的存在性；对Werner–Holevo信道族确定sharp私密容量与antidegradability阈值，并在非degradable范围算出精确complementary capacities；将Pauli half-erasure激活扩展到所有$1/2\le p<1$并给出新的四能级族；reference-assisted measured-$\chi^2$ witness解释了这些激活构造缺少complete comparison。

Conclusion: measured-$\chi^2$（及其Hermitian平滑与参考辅助版本）是连接entropic channel orders与私密容量激活现象的有效工具，可给出有限码可靠性–保密性界，并系统判定信道族的私密容量、antidegradability阈值与激活范围。

Abstract: Recent examples have shown that zero private capacity need not imply antidegradability and that two channels with zero private capacity can nevertheless transmit private information together. Existing entropic channel orders compare what a receiver and its environment can learn and thereby bound capacities. We connect these orders to the recent constructions through binary estimation, whose minimum mean-square error is governed by measured $χ^2$ divergence. A new integral representation shows how measured $χ^2$ on a qubit extension recovers quantum $χ^2$. It lets us pass from complete measured-$χ^2$ ordering to complete quantum-$χ^2$, relative-entropy, and less-noisy ordering. Zhu and Wang used a signed lift to show that their qutrit channel has zero private capacity. We show that Hermitian-smoothed measured-$χ^2$ ordering characterizes when such lifts exist, even with a quantum reference. Environmental dominance in binary estimation at every blocklength yields a finite-code reliability--secrecy bound. These results explain why complete comparison prevents activation with antidegradable helpers whereas regularized comparison alone does not. Building on that qutrit example, we establish this stability for a range of noisy Werner--Holevo channels, determine sharp private-capacity and antidegradability thresholds, and compute exact complementary capacities in a nondegradable range. By contrast, we extend Pauli half-erasure activation to all $1/2\le p<1$ and establish the same range for a new four-level family. Reference-assisted measured-$χ^2$ witnesses show why these activating constructions lack complete comparison.

</details>


### [137] [How to Build Pseudorandom Unitaries in Microcrypt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03711)
*Aditya Gulati,Dakshita Khurana,Kabir Tomer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文相对经典预言机给出首个证据：伪随机酉（PRU）可以存在而无需量子可计算单向函数；通过构造 F5HF4HF3HF2HF1 相位层电路，并证明其在经典随机预言机下可抵御 QMA 辅助的 BQP 对手。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已有工作（Kretschmer、Qian、Tal，STOC 2025）给出经典预言机下 PRU 的分离证据，但仅针对较弱对手。本文旨在强化该结果，证明 PRU 可抵御能反演单向函数、求解可高效验证单向谜题的 QMA 辅助对手，从而在经典预言机模型下提供更强的黑盒分离证据。

Method: 1) 构造五层独立随机对角相位层与 Hadamard 变换交替的电路 U=F5HF4HF3HF2HF1，证明其对酉及其逆、转置、复共轭的自适应受控访问下是强 PRU；2) 将相位层用经典随机预言机 O 实现，证明其对具有相干访问经典输入 QMA^PH^O 决策问题合适布尔完备的均匀 BQP 对手仍安全。

Result: 证明了五层相位+Hadamard 电路是强 PRU；在经典随机预言机实现下，该构造对能反演量子可计算单向函数、求解可高效验证单向谜题的 QMA 辅助均匀 BQP 对手保持安全，形成 PRU 不蕴含量子可计算单向函数的黑盒分离证据。

Conclusion: 显著强化了 Kretschmer 等人的经典预言机结果，获得可抵御 QMA 辅助对手的 PRU；构造简单高效、仅查询随机函数，为通过随机预言机启发式实现具体方案提供了路径。

Abstract: We provide the first evidence relative to a classical oracle that pseudorandom unitaries (PRUs) can exist without quantum-computable one-way functions.
  To obtain this result, we first prove that five independent random diagonal phase layers interleaved with Hadamard transforms \[ U=F_5HF_4HF_3HF_2HF_1 \] form a strong PRU with security under adaptive, controlled access to the unitary, its inverse, transpose, and complex conjugate.
  Our main result is that, when the phase layers are implemented using a classical random oracle O, the construction remains secure against uniform BQP adversaries with coherent access to suitable Boolean completions of classical-input $QMA^{PH}^{O}}$ decision problems. Such an adversary can invert (even quantum-computable) one-way functions and solve efficiently verifiable one-way puzzles defined relative to O. Our result therefore provides classical-oracle evidence that PRUs do not imply quantum-computable one-way functions, in the form of a black-box separation.
  We substantially strengthen the classical-oracle result of Kretschmer, Qian, and Tal (STOC 2025) by obtaining PRUs secure against these QMA-aided adversaries. Our construction also consists of a simple, efficient circuit that queries a random function, suggesting a route to concrete implementation via the random-oracle heuristic.

</details>


### [138] [SpiderCSS: Scalable Fault-Tolerant CSS State Preparation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03714)
*Boldizsár Poór,Andrey Boris Khesin,Sarah Meng Li,Benjamin Rodatz,John van de Wetering,Richie Yeung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SpiderCSS：基于ZX演算与容错等价重写的可扩展编译流水线，为任意CSS码生成高度优化的容错态制备电路，无需昂贵验证即可证明容错性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错逻辑态制备是大规模容错量子计算的关键原语。现有可扩展与启发式方法在电路深度、量子比特占用及逻辑错误率上仍有优化空间，且容错性验证代价高昂。

Method: 从CSS态的理想化规格出发，利用ZX演算和容错等价重写构造态制备图，保持初始理想ZX图的容错性；再以模块化构造生成容错CSS态制备电路，该构造在各组件内部及组件互连处均实现CNOT最小化。

Result: 在距离至多15的多种CSS码上的蒙特卡洛模拟表明，SpiderCSS可高效扩展，与现有可扩展和启发式方法相比显著降低电路深度与量子比特占用，同时逻辑错误率平均降低33.7%、接受率平均提升9.5%。

Conclusion: SpiderCSS通过仅使用容错等价重写，无需昂贵验证即可证明最终电路容错，并在深度、比特占用、逻辑错误率与接受率上综合优于现有方法，适用于任意CSS码的规模化容错态制备。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant preparation of logical states is a critical primitive for the realization of large-scale fault-tolerant quantum computing. This paper introduces SpiderCSS, a scalable compilation pipeline that generates highly-optimized, fault-tolerant state preparation circuits for arbitrary Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes. Starting from an idealized specification of a CSS state, we use the ZX-calculus and fault-equivalent rewrites to construct a diagram that prepares the given state while preserving the fault tolerance of the initial ideal ZX-diagram. From this diagram, SpiderCSS constructs a fault-tolerant CSS-state preparation circuit using a modular construction that is CNOT-minimal both within each component and also in the way these components are connected together. By only using fault-equivalent rewrites, the final circuit is provably fault-tolerant without needing expensive verification. Monte Carlo simulations across a variety of CSS codes up to distance 15 show that SpiderCSS scales efficiently and constructs circuits with substantially reduced depth and qubit footprint compared to existing scalable and heuristic methods, while simultaneously achieving an average decrease of 33.7% in logical error rate and an increase of 9.5% in acceptance rate.

</details>


<div id='physics.app-ph'></div>

# physics.app-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [139] [Lithium Niobate Surface-Acoustic-Wave Resonator for Very-High-Frequency Isolated Power Conversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02742)
*Liyang Jin,Zhiyang Cao,Zichen Xi,Jun Ji,Yizheng Zhu,Liyan Zhu,Linbo Shao*

Main category: physics.app-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种铌酸锂声表面波（SAW）谐振器，实现无磁隔离功率传输，在40 MHz下输出25.7 W、隔离电压2.17 kV，DC-AC转换效率达93.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压电器件可作为高频电力电子中无磁隔离能量传输的有前景方案，但现有压电器件在功率应用中面临工作频率、功率传输优值（FOM）、功率处理能力与电气隔离等方面的挑战，亟需改进。

Method: 设计面向应用的40 MHz铌酸锂SAW谐振器，采用两个电气隔离的叉指换能器（IDT）与声学布拉格反射镜结构；通过背面被动散热实现功率处理，并在输出IDT连接二极管整流器实现DC-DC转换。

Result: 谐振器在仅背面被动散热下实现25.7 W输出功率、温升低于15 °C，隔离电压2.17 kV；DC-AC转换峰值效率93.8%，功率传输FOM为13.47 mW/V²，峰值芯片功率密度81.7 W/cm³；DC-DC转换效率降至52.7%。

Conclusion: 该结果推动SAW器件成为下一代甚高频、小型化、高密度、无磁隔离功率变换器的核心组件之一。

Abstract: Piezoelectric devices offer a promising magnetic-free alternative for high-frequency power electronics, enabling compact energy transfer with inherent galvanic isolation. Here we demonstrate a lithium niobate surface acoustic wave (SAW) resonator that enables isolated power transfer and addresses the challenges in existing piezoelectric devices for power applications, including operation frequencies, power delivery figure of merit (FOM), power handling, and electrical isolation. Our application-optimized 40-MHz SAW resonator, with the two electrically isolated interdigital transducers (IDTs) and acoustic Bragg mirrors, achieves a 25.7 W output power at a less than 15 °C temperature rise with only passive cooling through the back mounting side, and an isolation voltage of 2.17 kV. In DC-AC power conversion, we demonstrate a peak efficiency of 93.8 %, a power delivery FOM of 13.47 mW/V$^2$ and a peak chip power density of 81.7 W/cm$^3$. DC-DC conversion is also demonstrated by connecting a diode rectifier to the output IDT, despite efficiency degrading to 52.7 %. Our results advance SAW devices as one of the core components for next-generation very-high-frequency, miniaturized, high-density, magnetic-free, isolated power converters.

</details>


<div id='cs.SD'></div>

# cs.SD [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [GAANet: Global-guided Asymmetric Attention Network for Audio-Visual Speech Separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02752)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Jingyuan Xu,Yiming Tang,Liu Liu,Dan Guo*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 提出GAANet，通过非对称多尺度融合与全局引导注意力实现高效音视频语音分离，在LRS2和VoxCeleb2上达到SOTA且轻量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音视频语音分离的多尺度方法容量受限，原因在于：1）对不同模态特征采用相同处理方式；2）忽略全局特征的作用。

Method: GAANet包含两项核心设计：一是非对称多尺度融合框架，让音频与视觉流在各自最优时间分辨率上提取并交互特征，无需对称时间下采样；二是全局引导注意力机制，将各模态压缩为时间维度为1的紧凑全局token，以其高层语义线索引导跨尺度的模态内与模态间融合。

Result: 在LRS2和VoxCeleb2上取得SOTA：LRS2达16.5 dB SI-SNRi，VoxCeleb2达14.0 dB；模型轻量，仅3.3M参数、19.8G MACs。

Conclusion: 结果验证了非对称时间建模与全局引导在高效、鲁棒多模态融合中的潜力；代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-scale design is crucial for efficient audio-visual speech separation, yet effectively modeling multi-scale information for audio-visual feature fusion remains challenging. We argue that the limited capacity of existing approaches primarily arises from: 1) treating features from different modalities in the same manner, and 2) overlooking the role of global features. To address these issues, we propose a Global-guided Asymmetric Attention Network (GAANet). Our model introduces two core innovations: first, an asymmetric multi-scale fusion framework that allows audio and visual streams to extract and interact with features at their respective optimal temporal resolutions, removing the need for symmetric temporal downsampling; second, a global-guided attention mechanism that compresses each modality into a compact global token with a temporal dimension of one, which then provides high-level semantic cues to guide both intra- and inter-modal fusion across scales. Experiments on LRS2 and VoxCeleb2 demonstrate that GAANet achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching 16.5 dB SI-SNRi on LRS2 and 14.0 dB on VoxCeleb2, while maintaining a lightweight computational profile with only 3.3M parameters and 19.8G MACs. These results highlight the strong potential of asymmetric temporal modeling and global guidance for efficient and robust multimodal fusion. The source code is publicly accessible at https://github.com/redizzy/GAANet

</details>


### [141] [What Actually Makes Correlation-Based SSL Distillation Noise-Robust? A Mechanistic Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02823)
*Fabian Ritter-Gutierrez,Nancy F. Chen,Eng Siong Chng*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文通过无偏全维探针、Pearson方差的分解、同噪声因果对照与逐维分析，证明在自监督语音模型的知识蒸馏中，真正带来噪声鲁棒性的是互相关的对角项，而非此前被认为起决定作用的自相关项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督语音模型精度高但参数量大；知识蒸馏可压缩模型，却使学生丧失教师的噪声鲁棒性。基于相关性的蒸馏方法包含互相关（对齐师生表征）与自相关（去相关学生特征）两项，原方法及De'HuBERT均将噪声鲁棒性归功于自相关项，但未将该作用分离验证，本文据此提出质疑。

Method: 在LibriSpeech-100上做蒸馏，采用留出的10 dB CHiME-3噪声；使用无偏的全维线性探针、Pearson方差分解、同噪声因果对照实验以及逐维分析来分离两项各自的贡献。

Result: 互相关的对角项是促使噪声不变性的机制，使噪声分类准确率从76.98%降至55.02%；再加入自相关项后仅维持在55.55%。自相关项几乎不消除噪声，而是重组特征空间、提升干净下游任务准确率；该结论在九个语音与音乐任务上一致。

Conclusion: 提出了明确的设计准则：为噪声鲁棒性应加权互相关项，为下游性能应调节自相关项，并独立采样教师与学生的噪声。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) speech models are accurate but large. Knowledge distillation compresses them, but the student loses the teacher's noise robustness. Correlation-based distillation addresses this with two terms: cross-correlation aligning student and teacher's representations, and self-correlation decorrelating the student's features. Both the original method and De'HuBERT credited the self-correlation term without isolating it. We show the opposite. On LibriSpeech-100 distillation with held-out CHiME-3 noise at 10\,dB, an unbiased full-dimensional probe, a Pearson-variance decomposition, a same-noise causal control, and a per-dimension analysis identify the cross-correlation diagonal as the mechanism that encourages noise invariance, lowering noise-classification accuracy from 76.98\% to 55.02\%, whereas adding the self-correlation term back leaves it at 55.55\%. The self-correlation term instead reorganises the feature space and improves accuracy on the clean downstream tasks, but removes essentially no noise. Across nine speech and music tasks this yields a concrete design rule for distillation: weight the cross-correlation term for noise robustness, tune the self-correlation term for better downstream performance, and sample teacher and student noise independently.

</details>


### [142] [Correlation-Based Distillation Yields More Mergeable Speech-Music Encoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02836)
*Fabian Ritter-Gutierrez*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 将蒸馏损失由DistilHuBERT的Lkd替换为相关性损失Lcl后，语音与音乐双教师蒸馏出的两个小学生模型在权重插值和通道置换两种合并算法下均表现更好，且改进源于Lcl产生的表征本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建同时覆盖语音和音乐的单一紧凑编码器可免除按领域分别维护模型的开销。现有做法是将语音教师与音乐教师分别蒸馏为小学生模型后再合并，合并算法有共享初始化下的权重插值与通道对齐置换后平均两种，但此前工作均固定蒸馏损失，未考察损失函数是否影响合并效果。

Method: 在架构与评测固定不变的前提下，把DistilHuBERT的蒸馏损失Lkd替换为基于相关性的损失Lcl，并在两种合并算法下对照评估：(1) 权重插值——比较学生在插值路径上相对各自更优端点的接近程度及语音任务表现；(2) 激活置换——比较需重排通道的层数、匹配通道的相关强度及语音任务表现。

Result: 权重插值下，Lcl学生在18组比较中有17组更接近自身更优端点，并在每个插值权重处均领先语音任务。激活置换下，Lcl学生在三层上需重排的通道更少，匹配通道相关性更强，语音任务同样领先。

Conclusion: 两种合并算法机制上互不共享，却在同一损失替换下同时获益，说明性能提升来源于Lcl所训练出的表征，而非某一种合并算法本身。

Abstract: A single compact encoder for both speech and music removes the need to maintain a separate model per domain. A practical recipe distils a speech teacher and a music teacher into two small students, then merges them into one model. Two merging algorithms exist: interpolating the student weights from a shared initialisation, or permuting their channels into alignment before averaging. Prior work fixed the distillation loss in both, leaving open whether the loss itself affects how well the students merge. We show that it does. Holding architecture and evaluation fixed, we replace the DistilHuBERT loss \Lkd{} with a correlation-based loss \Lcl{}. Under weight interpolation, \Lcl{} students stay closer to their own better endpoint in $17$ of $18$ comparisons and lead on the speech tasks at every interpolated weight. Under activation permutation, they need fewer channels rearranged at three layers, their matched channels correlate more strongly, and they again lead on speech. The two merging algorithms share no mechanism, yet both improve under the same loss substitution. This suggests that the gain lies in the representation \Lcl{} produces rather than in either algorithm.

</details>


### [143] [How Far Back Should a Transformer Look? Repetition and Copying in Music Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02837)
*Amir Fathi*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 研究符号音乐自回归模型的预测性能如何随最大上下文长度变化，发现长上下文收益主要来自精确复制：在重复密集的Nottingham/O'Neill's数据集上，NLL随上下文显著下降且可被复制基线解释94%；而在重复较少的MAESTRO/MusicNet上收益较小且96 tokens即基本饱和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究自回归符号音乐模型的预测性能对可用最大上下文的依赖，以及长上下文模型究竟利用了何种信息，并厘清此前"16 tokens即饱和"结论中可能存在的实验方法问题。

Method: 训练一个小型因果Transformer，分别在T∈{6,18,48,96,192,336}各上下文长度下独立训练，在相同目标位置上评估，比较原始token与非重叠二元隐码两种表示；通过引入/覆盖目标16-token历史的先前出现、施加无关扰动及简单复制基线进行归因分析；对训练好的336-token模型在测试时限制其历史；并在MAESTRO与MusicNet上做对照；同时审计早期报告的实验设定。

Result: Nottingham folk tunes上token预测器测试NLL在6到336 tokens间下降71%（0.986 bits/token），O'Neill's同样下降71%（302首测试曲中的129首）；大部分长上下文收益由精确复制解释：当目标16-token历史的早期出现进入上下文时性能提升，覆盖该出现则收益消失，而同等规模无关扰动不消失，简单复制基线可恢复94%的下降；336-token模型在测试时限制为近期历史也会失去大部分收益。MAESTRO与MusicNet上重复显著较少，降幅较小（9%与18%）且96 tokens基本达到。审计发现早期草稿的饱和结论源于划分泄漏、重叠隐感受野、平均化预测器和逐文件tempo网格。

Conclusion: 符号音乐上的长上下文预测收益主要来源于对精确重复片段的复制，因此依赖于所渲染的乐谱表示（包含展开的反复记号）；在重复较少的演奏/乐谱数据集上收益有限且很快饱和。上下文长度评测需警惕数据划分泄漏、隐表示感受野重叠等实验方法陷阱。

Abstract: We investigate how predictive performance depends on the maximum context available to an autoregressive model of symbolic music, and what information long-context models exploit. A small causal Transformer is trained separately at each context length T in {6, 18, 48, 96, 192, 336} and evaluated on the same target positions, both over raw tokens and over non-overlapping binary latent codes. On Nottingham folk tunes, the token predictor's test NLL decreases by 71% (0.986 bits per token) between 6 and 336 tokens, and by 71% on the O'Neill's tunes long enough for the same sweep (129 of 302 test tunes). Most of the long-context gain is explained by exact copying: the improvement appears when an earlier occurrence of the target's 16-token history enters the available context; overwriting that occurrence removes the gain, whereas equally large unrelated corruption does not; and a simple copy baseline recovers 94% of the reduction. A trained 336-token model likewise loses most of this gain when its history is restricted to recent tokens at test time. Because many repeats arise from written repeat signs expanded in the score, this result pertains specifically to these rendered score representations. By contrast, on MAESTRO performances and MusicNet scores, where exact repeats are substantially less frequent, the reduction is smaller (9% and 18%) and is largely attained by 96 tokens. Finally, an audit of an earlier draft that reported saturation at 16 tokens identified split leakage, overlapping latent receptive fields, an averaging predictor, and a per-file tempo grid; we present these as methodological checks for context-length measurements.

</details>


### [144] [Learning Jazz Pianist Style with Cross-Attention Conditioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.02918)
*Drew Edwards,Akira Maezawa,Simon Dixon*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文利用预训练符号音乐Transformer研究爵士钢琴家风格，证明其表示能编码演奏者身份，并通过交叉注意力身份嵌入实现风格条件生成，两类评估显示生成结果具有可辨识的风格结构，并可用于定位代表性音乐片段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 爵士钢琴家的个人风格虽能被有经验的听众快速识别，但其潜在特征难以形式化描述，因此需要一个可计算、可验证的框架来刻画并生成演奏者风格。

Method: 基于预训练符号音乐Transformer，先验证其学习表示对钢琴家身份的编码能力并进行分类；再引入对学习到的钢琴家身份嵌入的交叉注意力机制，使模型能够按特定艺术家风格进行条件生成；最后复用分类器以滑动窗口方式定位演奏中最具代表性的时刻。

Result: 在两个基准上实现高精度钢琴家身份分类；滑动窗口分类器对条件续写能稳定归因到正确艺术家，远高于无条件基线；仅用合成生成训练的12类分类器在真实钢琴家上达到87%片段级和95%整曲级准确率。

Conclusion: 预训练符号音乐Transformer的表示已隐含演奏者身份信息，结合身份嵌入条件生成可捕捉有意义的风格结构，并能揭示区分不同钢琴家声音的具体音乐姿态，为音乐风格建模与可解释分析提供路径。

Abstract: Jazz pianists develop distinctive traits that experienced listeners can often identify within seconds, yet the features underlying this recognition resist formal description. We study jazz pianist style through the lens of a pretrained symbolic music transformer, showing that its learned representations already encode pianist identity well enough for highly accurate classification across two benchmarks. We then augment the transformer with cross-attention over learned pianist identity embeddings, enabling it to generate music conditioned on a specific artist's style. Two evaluation protocols confirm that the generator captures meaningful stylistic structure: a sliding-window classifier consistently attributes conditioned continuations to the correct artist, far above unconditioned baselines; and a classifier trained entirely on synthetic generations identifies real pianists across 12 classes with 87% chunk-level and 95% song-level accuracy. Finally, we repurpose the classifier to locate the most characteristic moments within a performance, surfacing the specific musical gestures that distinguish each pianist's voice.

</details>


### [145] [ParaGeo: Decomposing Paralinguistic Variation into a Shared Latent Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03125)
*Yuhan Liu,Yuxuan Ou,Ruoxi Su,Mohamed Ahmed Zaki,Yunbo Long*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 该论文提出ParaGeo，通过在冻结的语音语言模型中对副语言变异进行"同内容分解"，得到跨属性共享的低维坐标表示，并验证其可分离性与可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语音表现同时受所请求的副语言属性与语言内容影响，二者混杂，难以在隐空间中单独度量副语言结构，也缺乏可控语音生成的实证起点。

Method: 在冻结语音语言模型上，对合成音频token以固定聆听提示重放，将池化K/V表示中心化后投影到共享低维空间；基于全局拟合的校准基，采用内容留出的质心准确率与同类跨内容余弦相似度度量，并做置换检验；此外用十场景六风格探针及静态、加性、时序干预分析响应。

Result: GLM-4-Voice探针覆盖12个基准族、80个请求控制项与8个句子。内容留出质心准确率9.49%，置换基线1.25%；同类跨内容余弦相似度0.285对0.017，两项条件置换检验均p=0.001。十场景六风格探针显示跨场景可复现的对比方向；静态、加性、时序干预呈现属性、层与调度依赖的响应曲线。

Conclusion: 结果为副语言结构提供了共享坐标表示与度量方法，并为潜在语音控制提供实证起点；代码已开源。

Abstract: Speech delivery varies with both the requested paralinguistic attribute and the linguistic content. We introduce ParaGeo, a matched-content decomposition of paralinguistic variation in a frozen speech language model. Synthesized audio tokens are replayed with a fixed listening prompt; pooled key/value (K/V) representations are centered and projected into a shared low-dimensional space. Our GLM-4-Voice probe spans 80 requested controls from 12 benchmark families across eight sentences. With a globally fitted calibration basis, content-held-out centroid accuracy using this basis is 9.49% versus a 1.25% permutation baseline; same-label cross-content cosine similarity is 0.285 versus 0.017, and both conditional permutation tests yield p = 0.001. A separate ten-scenario, six-style probe reveals reproducible contrast directions across scenarios. Static, additive, and temporal interventions produce attribute-, layer-, and schedule-dependent response profiles. These results provide a shared coordinate representation for measuring paralinguistic structure and an empirical starting point for latent speech control. Code is available at https://github.com/yuhanlydia/ParaGeo.

</details>


### [146] [DriftTTS: Few-Step Text-to-Speech Without Distillation via Distribution-Matching Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03390)
*Mohammad Nur Hossain Khan,Subrata Biswas,Bashima Islam*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: DriftTTS：一种无需生成式教师、蒸馏或对抗判别的少步梅尔谱生成器，采用分布匹配漂移目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步神经TTS依赖缩短的扩散/流匹配调度，或从预训练多步教师蒸馏，存在额外依赖负担。

Method: 在由原始梅尔谱与冻结的掩码自编码器编码器（在同一LJSpeech训练集上预训练）定义的梅尔域特征空间中，使用分布匹配漂移目标；采用on-policy rollout，让解码器在自身中间状态上训练，支持训练深度内的推理。

Result: LJSpeech上NFE=4时MCD 3.87 dB、WER 3.7%（Matcha-TTS为3.85 dB、3.4%）；配对盲听测试MOS 4.18，Matcha-TTS 3.96，真值4.22。

Conclusion: 证明了无需预训练生成式教师即可实现具有竞争力的少步语音合成。

Abstract: Few-step neural text-to-speech models often rely on short- ened diffusion or flow-matching schedules, or on distillation from pretrained multi-step teachers. To avoid these depen- dencies, we present DriftTTS, a few-step mel-spectrogram generator trained without a generative teacher, distillation, or adversarial discrimination. DriftTTS uses a distribution- matching drift objective in a mel-domain feature space defined by raw mels and a frozen masked-autoencoder encoder pretrained on the same LJSpeech training split. On-policy rollout trains the decoder on its own interme- diate states and supports inference up to the trained roll- out depth. On LJSpeech, DriftTTS at NFE=4 achieves 3.87 dB MCD and 3.7% WER, compared with 3.85 dB and 3.4% for Matcha-TTS. In a fully paired blind listen- ing test, DriftTTS obtains 4.18 MOS, compared with 3.96 for Matcha-TTS and 4.22 for ground truth. These results demonstrate competitive few-step synthesis without a pre- trained generative teacher. Code can be found at https: //github.com/BASHLab/driftTTS.git

</details>


### [147] [Harmonic Eigenspace: A Web-based Application for Navigating and Composing Microtonal Harmony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03398)
*David Dalmazzo,Ken Déguernel*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于Web的微音程和声导航与创作应用，构建于四维"和声特征空间"之上，通过Sethares粗糙度/不协和模型计算四音和弦的频谱不协和轮廓，将和弦类型定位为该空间中的方向，并嵌入三种音律的格点，结合3D可视化与53-TET模态工作室，兼具MPX MIDI控制器功能，并通过31人听测验证其可用性与接受度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微音程和声缺乏直观的导航与创作工具。作者希望以心理声学（不协和度）为基础，构建一个可演奏、可视化的连续空间，使作曲家能够在其中定位、比较并创作四音和弦与进行，尤其是将53-TET等音律的离散和弦格点嵌入连续的不协和体积中，从而弥合理论空间与实际创作之间的鸿沟。

Method: 1) 以Sethares粗糙度/不协和模型计算四音和弦的频谱不协和轮廓，构建四维和声特征空间；2) 采用移调不变的坐标三元组(α, β, γ)定位根音之上的三个音，使和弦性质对应空间中的方向（不变量射线），根音频率决定尺度；3) 在任意音区计算不协和场，定位其局部极小值（因分音重合比例条件而具音区不变性），共100个极小值对应纯律音程，构成地标并划分出以最协和四音和弦为中心的盆地；4) 将三种平均律的四音和弦嵌入为离散格点；5) 开发Web应用，含可演奏的3D不协和体积可视化与53-TET十级音程词汇的模态工作室；6) 支持MPE MIDI控制；7) 开展31人听测，评估孤立53-TET和弦与熟悉的西方和弦（如maj7、m7）以及模态工作室所作进行的接受度。

Result: 不协和体积包含100个局部极小值，与纯律音程对齐，其位置具音区不变性，将空间组织为以最协和四音和弦为核心的盆地结构。应用成功实现3D可视化与可演奏节点、53-TET模态工作室及MPE MIDI控制功能。31名参与者的听测中，听众对孤立53-TET和弦与西方熟悉和弦（maj7、m7）进行了评分，并对首次听到的微音程进行进行了接受/拒绝评价。

Conclusion: 该工作将心理声学不协和度模型转化为可导航、可演奏的微音程和声创作工具，通过移调不变坐标与53-TET格点嵌入，提供了兼具理论一致性与实用性的平台；听测结果为首次接触微音程进行的接受度提供了实证数据，验证了该应用在和声探索与创作中的可行性与潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a web-based application for navigating and composing microtonal harmony, built on the Harmonic Eigenspace, a four-dimensional psychoacoustically grounded space in which tetrad chord types are located by their spectral dissonance profiles, computed with Sethares's roughness/dissonance model. The coordinate system is transposition-invariant: a coordinate triple (α, \b{eta}, γ) locates the three upper notes in relation to the root, so a chord quality corresponds to a direction in the space, the invariant ray along which transposition acts, while the root frequency sets the scale. The dissonance field over these coordinates can be computed at any register; the locations of its local minima are register-invariant, as they arise from partial-coincidence ratio conditions. The dissonance volume contains 100 local minima that align with just-intonation intervals and act as landmarks, organising the space into basins around the most consonant tetrads. We embed tetrads from three tonal equal temperaments as discrete lattices within this continuous volume. The application presents this space through two components: the Harmonic Eigenspace as a navigable 3D visualisation of the dissonance volume in which all nodes are playable, and a Modal Studio that extends modal interchange logic to the ten-gradation interval vocabulary of 53-TET. The application also functions as a MIDI controller with MIDI Polyphonic Expression support, usable in any digital audio workstation that supports this format. A listening study with 31 participants used both scenes of the application: listeners first rated isolated 53-TET chords alongside chords familiar from Western practice, such as the maj7 and the m7; they then rated chord progressions composed in the Modal Studio, measuring their acceptance or rejection of microtonal progressions heard for the first time.

</details>


### [148] [PEACE: Joint Embeddings of DSP Effects Code and Audio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03405)
*David Braun,Adam Finkelstein*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出PEACE，首个音频效果代码与输出音频的联合嵌入模型，结合AFx-Rep音频编码器与Faust代码编码器（T5与图神经网络），在音频到代码检索任务上验证其拓扑与参数双重理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作缺乏对音频效果（DSP）代码与输出音频之间对应关系的建模，缺乏能在拓扑与参数层面理解效果链的联合表示，限制了面向DSP代码的MIR系统（检索、生成、条件控制）的发展。

Method: 以SLAP多模态目标为基础，将AFx-Rep音频编码器与两个Faust代码编码器配对：一是微调的T5 transformer处理Faust源码；二是消息传递图神经网络处理Faust编译器的中间表示，同时捕捉拓扑结构与UI参数。在音频到代码检索任务上评估，推理时掩蔽UI参数以获得只编码效果链拓扑的嵌入。

Result: 参数可见时，两个代码编码器在混合长度效果链检索上表现相当，但在单效果库检索上各有取舍；参数完全掩蔽时，PEACE能以远超随机的水平恢复有序链拓扑，且不受监督方法局限。PEACE在分布外混响检索基准上超过预训练模型，并能提升冻结的纯音频表示。

Conclusion: PEACE对效果链拓扑与参数的双重理解为可搜索、生成和以DSP代码为条件控制的音乐信息检索系统奠定了基础。

Abstract: This paper introduces PEACE, the first joint embedding of audio effect code and output audio. Building on SLAP's multimodal objective, we pair an AFx-Rep audio encoder with two code encoders for Faust, a functional language for audio signal processing. First, we evaluate a fine-tuned T5 transformer over Faust source code. Second, we evaluate a message-passing graph neural network over an intermediate representation of the Faust compiler, capturing both topology and UI parameters. We evaluate on audio-to-code retrieval, where masking UI parameters at inference yields embeddings that encode effect chain topology alone. When parameters are visible, the two code encoders tie on retrieval of mixed-length chains but have tradeoffs on single-effect galleries. With parameters fully masked, PEACE recovers ordered chain topology far above chance without the limitations of supervised methods. PEACE outperforms pretrained models on an out-of-distribution reverb retrieval benchmark and can improve frozen audio-only representations. Its dual understanding of topology and parameters lays the groundwork for music information retrieval systems that search, generate, and condition on DSP code.

</details>


### [149] [LayerIt: Towards a Framework for Time-Aligned, Composable Music Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03428)
*Fernando Azeredo,António Sá Pinto*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文提出 LayerIt，一个开源 Python 库，可将乐谱符号、信号特征与算法输出等多源表示统一对齐到同一演奏时间轴上，并输出单一 SVG，用于音乐信息检索的可视化分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 音乐信息检索常需在不同坐标系间关联信号、算法与符号信息，但现有可视化通常将乐谱符号与物理时间分离，组合图多依赖手工拼装，难以统一检视。因此需要一个能自动合成多表示、共享演奏时间轴的工具。

Method: LayerIt 接收经 warp 到演奏时间的乐谱及音符级对齐信息，把相互独立的表示层（notation、tracker 输出、信号表示等）组合在共享的 performance-time 轴上，输出单一 SVG；该 SVG 保留乐谱的 MEI 结构，且新增组件保持可识别、可重新样式化（restylable）。

Result: 作者以具有挑战性的节拍跟踪分析为例进行演示，将 tracker 输出、信号表示与乐谱符号同时呈现，从而能对照记谱结构与实际演奏时间定位节拍（metrical）不一致及其他错误。

Conclusion: LayerIt 提供了可复用、可扩展的开源方案，使符号、信号与算法结果能在统一演奏时间轴上协同可视化和核查，有助于音乐信息检索中的误差分析与验证。

Abstract: Music information retrieval often relates signal-derived, algorithmic, and symbolic information across different coordinate systems. Existing visualizations typically leave notation separate from physical time, while composites that combine them are assembled by hand. We present LayerIt, an open-source Python library for composing independent representations with notation on a shared performance-time axis. Given a score warped into performance time and a note-level alignment, LayerIt emits a single SVG that preserves the score's MEI structure and keeps added components identifiable and restylable. We demonstrate this through a challenging beat-tracking analysis, in which tracker output, signal representations, and notation can be inspected together to locate metrical disagreement and other errors against both notated structure and performed time.

</details>


### [150] [Rubric-Based Optimization for Text-to-Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03589)
*Ping Wang,Guang Yang,Shao-Rong Su,Junkai Wu,Pang Wei Koh,Noah A. Smith*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文研究将预训练音频-语言模型（ALM）产生的结构化评分（rubric）作为音乐生成后训练奖励，在自回归与扩散模型上分别用偏好对DPO和标量奖励DiffusionNFT优化，并比较ALM rubric与专用客观奖励的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 任何单一自动指标都难以全面刻画音乐质量的多维特性，后训练文本到音乐生成需要能够捕获多个音乐质量维度的奖励信号；因此需要探索更具结构性的奖励来源（ALM rubric）并明确其适用范围。

Method: 使用预训练音频-语言模型（ALM）按rubric对每个生成片段打分；对同一文本提示生成的候选按分数排序构造偏好对，在MusicGen-small与ACE-Step v1上进行DPO；同时把rubric分数直接作为标量奖励，在ACE-Step v1上做DiffusionNFT。另研究节奏、调性、配器三类有精确客观奖励的属性，与ALM rubric做对比。

Result: 在MusicCaps上，rubric优化同时提升CLAP、SongEval与Audiobox-Aesthetics，其中ACE-Step上的DiffusionNFT增益最强；而在MusicGen-small上，用任一自动评估器构造偏好都会出现明显的跨指标权衡——目标评估器提升，其他独立评估器退化。在节奏、调性与配器上，直接优化专用奖励能稳定提升目标属性；ALM rubric仅在节奏与配器上有部分迁移，对调性无可测提升。

Conclusion: 提出实用的分工原则：ALM rubric适合优化难以形式化的广泛感知质量；当存在可靠测量时，专用客观奖励仍更可取。

Abstract: Post-training text-to-music generation requires reward signals that capture multiple aspects of musical quality beyond what any single automatic metric can measure. We study structured, rubric-based rewards from pretrained audio-language models (ALMs) as training signals for both autoregressive and diffusion-based music generators. An ALM scores each generated clip against the rubric; we rank candidates generated for the same text prompt by their scores and convert these rankings into preference pairs for DPO on both MusicGen-small and ACE-Step~v1, and additionally use the rubric scores directly as scalar rewards for DiffusionNFT on ACE-Step~v1. On MusicCaps, rubric-based optimization improves CLAP, SongEval, and Audiobox-Aesthetics simultaneously, with the strongest gains obtained by DiffusionNFT on ACE-Step. By contrast, on MusicGen-small, building preferences from any one of these automatic evaluators produces clear cross-metric trade-offs: the targeted evaluator improves while other independent evaluators deteriorate. We further study tempo, key, and instrumentation, where precise objective rewards are available. Directly optimizing these specialized rewards reliably improves the target attributes, whereas ALM rubrics provide only partial transfer for tempo and instrumentation and no measurable improvement for key. Together, these results suggest a practical division of labor: ALM rubrics are effective for broad perceptual qualities that are difficult to formalize, while specialized objective rewards remain preferable when reliable measurements are available.

</details>


### [151] [Revisiting Input Time-frequency Representations in Multi-pitch Estimation for Vocal Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03656)
*Junyoung Koh,Hao-Wen Dong*

Main category: cs.SD

TL;DR: 本文比较了HCQT与线性STFT在合唱多音高估计中的表现，发现线性STFT在效果更好且特征提取成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合唱多音高估计中歌手音域重叠、基频接近，谐波在时频表示中重叠。现有方法常用HCQT提供频率自适应分辨率，但训练时动态生成混合信号导致特征提取开销大。作者希望重新审视该设计，寻找更高效的输入表示。

Method: 将HCQT与线性STFT进行对比，后者直接将频率bin作为模型输入；并进一步分析分析窗长度与频谱覆盖范围的影响，以及限制输入到预测音高范围的效果。

Result: 线性STFT优于HCQT，同时显著降低特征提取成本；更长的分析窗或更宽频谱覆盖无额外提升；限制输入到预测音高范围会削弱线性STFT的优势。

Conclusion: 更细的频率分辨率不必然提升合唱多音高估计性能，较短分析窗对时变人声音高可能更有效。

Abstract: Multi-pitch estimation in vocal ensembles is challenging because singers occupy overlapping pitch ranges and often sing at closely spaced fundamental frequencies, causing their harmonics to overlap in time-frequency representations. Existing models commonly use harmonic constant-Q transform (HCQT)-based representations to provide frequency-adaptive resolution, at the cost of expensive feature extraction when training mixtures are generated on the fly. We revisit this design and compare HCQT with a linear short-time Fourier transform (STFT), whose frequency bins are directly provided as model inputs. Despite its fixed frequency resolution and the absence of a pitch-aligned input grid, the linear STFT outperforms HCQT while substantially reducing feature-extraction cost. Further analysis shows that a longer analysis window or broader spectral coverage provides no additional improvement, while restricting the input to the predicted pitch range reduces the advantage of the linear STFT. These results suggest that finer frequency resolution does not necessarily improve vocal-ensemble MPE, and that shorter analysis windows can be more effective for time-varying vocal pitches.

</details>
